Date: 9-2-2004

# JUNIOR ASSISTANT OF SADASHIVAPET MUNICIPALITY ARRESTED IN BRIBE CASE

Mr. Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant, O/o Sadashivapet Municipality in Medak district was trapped by ACB Officials on 9-2-2004 when he demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs. 1,000/- from the complainant to do an official-favour of processing his application for Ownership Certificate. He tested positive during chemical test. The tainted amount was recovered from him. He is arrested and will be produced before Special Court for ACB Cases, Hyderabad.

The case is under investigation.

Sub:- Public Servants – Allegations of corruption -- Demand and acceptance of bribe against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Sadasivapct Municipality, Mcdak District --- Trapped on 9-2-2004 – Reg.

Ref: - 1. Govt. Circular Memo No.2045/Spl.B/2000-4, dated 25-5-2001 of Genl.Admn (Spl.B) Department

2. Govt.U.O.Notc No.1818/Spl.B/2000-4, dated 21-11-2001 of General Admn (Spl.B) Department

3. Govt. Memo. No. 19719/Ser.C/2003, dated 18-12-2003 of General Administration (Ser.C) Department.

4. Automex Message in Crime No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, dated 10-2-2004 of the Inspector of Police, A.C.B., Medak at Sangareddy.

A copy of the Automex Message  $ 3^{rd} $ cited, received in the Bureau intimating about the trap against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Sadasivapet Municipality, Medak District Medak District is enclosed herewith.

Further, it is informed that the Accused Officer was arrested on 9-2-2004 at 4.10 p.m., produced before the Principal Special Judge for S.P.E. & A.C.B. Cases, Hyderabad on 10-2-2004 at 10.30 a.m. and released on bail on 11-2-2004.

It is requested that the above Accused Officer may be placed under suspension immediately in consonance with standing instructions of Government

issued vide references $1^{st}$, $2^{nd}$ and $3^{rd}$ cited above and a copy of the suspension ordes may be furnished to the Bureau for record.

It is also requested that caveats may be filed through the Government Pleader concerned in the A.P. Administrative Tribunal and also in the High Court of A.P. with a view to restraining the Accused Officer from obtaining release orders from the A.P. Administrative Tribunal or the A.P. High Court.

This may be treated as Preliminary Report. Final Report will be sent in due course through the Vigilance Commissioner, A.P., Hyderabad.

#####  $ \underline{\text{Enclosures:}} $

Yours faithfully,

1. Copy of F.I.R.

2. Copies of Mediators' Reports I & II.

for Director General,

Anti-Corruption Burcau,

Hyderabad.

Sir,

Sub: - Public Servants - Allegation of corruption against Sri M. Janardan S/o Narsimulu, 53 yrs, Jr. Asst O/o Municipal Commissioner Sadasaivpet, Medak District, Trapped on 9-2-2004-Request to issue trap Confirmation orders - Reg

Ref; Cr.No. 5/ACB—NZB/2004 u/s 7 of The P.C. Act 1988.

##### & ఓ & ఓ

I Submit that, On 6-2-2004 at 10.00 AM the complainant Sri Bodapally Ravi s/o Shankarappa, 40 yrs, occ School Assistant, Sadasivpet town, Medak Dist, reported that he has been working as a school assistant in Govt Ravindra model school, Sadasivpet. His father Sri Badapally shankarappa 68 yrs is a retired teacher. They are having an own house bearing No 3-4-30, in yedla bazaar in ward no.11 of Sadasivpet municipality area. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the municipal office Sadasivpet for the issue of ownership certificate. Due to old age of his father the complainant approached concerned clerk in Sadasivpet Municipality office i.e. Sri Janardan and when asked about the application he demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later when he met janardan for certificate he asked him whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that your work would be done as and when you bring bribe. Unless you pay the bribe your application would not be processed. He informed the same to his father. His father is not willing to get his work done by giving bribe. He came to ACB office Sangareddy and requested to take necessary action against the clerk Sri Janardan who is working in the Municipal office of Sadasivpet.

39

Further I submit that after due verification of the genuineness of the complainant and the reputation of the accused officer and as per telephonic instructions issued by the kind officer a case mentioned in the reference was registered and investigated into. During the course of investigation the accused officer was successfully trapped on 9-2-2004 in the office of office of Municipal Commissioner Sadasivpet, Medak District. When he demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs 1000/- from the complainant.

Hence I request my kind officer to issue trap confirmation orders in the above case.

Yours faithfully,

(V. Vijaya Bhaskar) 18/12/2004

Inspector of police

A.C.B, Medak Dist.

At Sangareddy,

# PLAN OF ACTION OF P.RAMACHANDRA RAO, INSPECTOR OF POLICE, ANTI-CORRUPTION BUREAU, NIZAMABAD RANGE, NIZAMABAD, IN CR.NO.5/ACB- NZB/2004, AGAINST SRI.MAMDI JANARDHAN, JUNIOR ASSISTANT, O/O MUNICIPAL COMMISSIONER, SADASHIVAPET, MEDAK DISTRICT.

### 1. Case File Number

### 2. Allegation

### 5/ACB-NZB/2004.

### 3. Oral Evidence

1. That Sri. Mamidi Janardhan, S/o Narsimulu, age;53, Occ: Junior Assistant, O/o Municipal Commissioner, Sadashivapet, Medak District, demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant Sri. B. Ravi S/o Shankarappa, age: 40 yrs, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivapet Town, Medak District for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house owner ship certificate.

I. The I.O. has already examined and recorded the statements of the following witnesses.

a. Sri. Bodapalli Ravi S/o Shankarappa, Age:40 Yrs, Occ: Teacher, r/o. Sadashivapet, Medak District.

b. Sri.R.Kistaiah, S/o Balaiah, Age:53 Yrs, Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak district.

### 4. Documentary Evidence

c. Sri.D. Satyanrayana, s/o D.Laxmaiah, age;58 yrs, Junior Assistant, O/o Municipal Commissioner, Sadashivapet, Medak district.

II. The I.O. will examine and record the statements of the following witnesses.

a. Sri. Bodapalli Shankarappa, S/o Bakappa, Age:68

yrs, Occ: Retd. Teacher, r/o Sadashivapet,

Medak District.

b. Smt. Pankajam, former Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak district.

III. The I.O will get the statements of the complainant recorded U/s 164 Cr.P.C.

IV. The I.O. will examine any other witnesses whose evidence is relevant to the case.

I. The I.O. will collect the following documentary evidence.

1. The I.O. has already collected the application given by the complainant's father, office note written by the A.O. and etc.,

2. The I.O. has already collected 1) One Miscellaneous receipt book, 2) Local Tappa Register, and 3) Attendance Register of office of Municipal Commissioner, Sadashivapet, Medak District.

3. The I.O. will collect the Service Particulars of the A.O from the Municipal Commissioner, Sadashivapet, Medak District.

4. The I.O. will serve notice on the A.O and get his explanation.

5. The I.O. will collect any other document/records relevant to the case.

The I.O will complete investigation duly covering all the aspects and submit D.F.R within the stipulated time.

Whereas it has come to the notice of the undersigned alleging that Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality has trapped by the ACB personals.

And whereas disciplinary proceedings against Sri. M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet are contemplated.

And whereas the undersigned after careful consideration of the available material and having due regard to the circumstances of the case is satisfied that it is necessary and in public interest to place Sri. M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality under suspension.

Now, Therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Sub-rule(1) of Rule 8 of A.P.Civil Services (CCA) Rules, 1991 and Rule 28 (3) of APMMSS rules issued in G.O.Ms.No. 292 M.A., dt. 16-5-92 the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration Hyderabad hereby places the said Sri.M.Janrdhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality under suspension from the date of communication of this order and he shall continue to be under

suspension until the conclusion of the disciplinary proceedings/termination of all proceedings relating to the criminal charges.

It is further ordered that during the period of this order remains in force, the headquarters of Sri.M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, shall not leave the headquarters without obtaining the previous permission of the undersigned.

Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality is eligible for drawl of subsistence allowance as per rules during the period of suspension.

FINAL REPORT

(LEGAL OPINION)

01.Crime No. ___5/ACB-NZB/2004

02.Name and Designation of the AO :: SRI M.JANARDHAN

JUNIOR ASST.,

MUNICIPAL COUNCIL

SADASHIVAPET

MEDAK DIST.,

05.Date of working of AO in Dept., :: 22.2.2004

06.Name of the I.O.

SRI P. RAMACHANDRA RAO

INSPECTOR OF POLICE

ACB, NIZAMABAD RANGE

07. Whether the AO working on depu-:: PERMANENTation or on regular basis

08. Name of the Legal Officer

SMT.M.RADHIKA

SPECIAL P.P.,

ANTI-CORRUPTION BUREAU

HYDERABAD.

09.a)Date of receipt of FR-I :: 15.3.2004

b)Date of despatch of FR-II : 7.4.2004

c) Reasons for delay

∴ There is no delay

10. NATURE OF ALLEGATION:

That Sri M. Janardhan, Jr. Asst., Municipal Council., Sadashivpet, Medak Dist., demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- on 9.2.2004 at his office from the complainant Sri Bodapally Ravi, Teacher for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house ownership certificate.

### 11. ANALYSIS OF THE CASE:

I have gone through the Final Report Part-I submitted by Sri P.Ramachandra Rao, Inspector, ACB, Nizamabad Range and I finalize my part-II report as follows:-

The brief facts of the case are that the complainant Sri B.Ravi working as School Asst. in Govt. Ravindra Model School, Sadasivpet and his father Sri B.Shankarappa was a retired teacher and that his father gave an application dated 02.1.2004 in the Municipal Office, Sadashivpet for the issue of house ownership certificate. On behalf of his father the complainant approached Sri Janardhan, Clerk in the municipal council office for house ownership certificate. Sri Janardhan, A.O demanded an amount of Rs.1000/- as bribe to process the application submitted by his father. The AO also gave instructions to the complainant when he approached that as and when the bribe amount is paid his work would be done and unless the bribe is paid their application would not be processed. The same was informed to the father of the complainant and the father of the complainant was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe. Hence, the complainant Sri Ravi approached the Inspector of Police, ACB, Nizamabad on 6.2.2004 and lodged a complaint against 0the AO.

After causing discreet enquiries a case inCr.Npo.5/ACB-NZB/04 U/s 7 of PC Act 1988 was registered on 9.2.2004.

The Inspector secured the presence of Sri Padma Narayana and Sri Tammala Shivaiah to act as mediators.

Note file pertaining to the father of the complainant containing 1 to 5 sheets seized from the possession of the AO on 9.2.2004, the miscellaneous

receipt book containing the receipt written by the AO in the name of the father of the AO, the inward register containing page Nos. 1 to 170 wherein the particulars of the application submitted by the complainant's father were entered in page No.153 vide S.No.324 dated 2.1.2004 will prove the fact that there is an official favour pending in between the AO and the complainant. The statement of the complainant, the complaint coupled with the first mediators report is sufficient to prove that when the complainant requested about the issue of house ownership certificate the AO demanded a bribe of Rs.1000/- to do official favour. The statement of the complainant and the 2nd mediators report will prove the fact on 9.2.2004 as per the demand of the AO the complainant paid the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- to the AO. The resultant solutions of the right hand fingers of the AO, the inner flap of the right side pant pocket of the AO which yielded positive results is sufficient to say that the AO had accepted the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- from the complainant. The seized amount and the post trap proceedings is sufficient to prove the recovery.

The subsequent explanation of AO that he had neither demanded nor accepted any bribe from the complainant can not be believed since the AO has received the application of the complainant on 4.1.2004 and it was kept pending from 4.1.2004 to 9.2.2004 for more than one month and on 9.2.2004 after receiving the bribe amount the AO delivered the house owner certificate to the complainant. The oral and documentary evidence and the positive results of the hand wash and inner flap of right side pant pocket is sufficient to say that the AO had demanded and accepted the bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant Sri B.Ravi on 9.2.2004 for showing official favour in processing the application of complainant's father for issue of house owners certificate.

### 12.FINAL RECOMMENDATION OF THE LEGAL OFFICER:-

In view of the oral and documentary evidence collected by the I.O it is sufficient to prosecute the AO in a court of law for the offences punishable under section 7,11,13(2) r/w 13(1)(d) of PC Act 1988.

SPECIAL P.P.,

ANTI-CORRUPTION BUREAU

HYDERABAD.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//250460d9-e71d-4553-8bcc-5fa9a1e6381b/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_image_box_191_1138_744_1404.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A16Z%2F-1%2F%2F50ac963a3e2357faaeb4186efcf0d9e2dd02b4cb5c199699e1484619ea0ef1a0" alt="Image" width="16%" /></div>

*****

That AO Sri M. Janardhan, Jr. Asst. Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak Dist demanded and accepted an amount of Rs.1,000/- as bribe on 19-2-2004 at his office from the complainant Sri Bodapalli Ravi, Teacher for showing official favour of processing the application of complainant's father for issue of house ownership certificate.

This can be proved with the complaint of complainant, FIR, his 161, 164 Cr.P.C. Statements, 161 Cr.P.C. Statement of complainant's father, Mediators Report I & II, Note File, Receipt Book, Inward Register etc. seized on 19-2-2004, evidence of Mediators and ACB staff will establish the guilt of A.O. Chemical test conducted on the right hand fingers of AO and on the inner flap of the right side pocket of AO yielded positive.

The very fact that AO kept pending the work of complainant from 2-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 is proof of malafide intention of AO. Evidence on record is sufficient to prove the guilt of AO beyond doubt.

Hence, it is recommended for prosecution of AO in a Court of law.

Joint of law.

Joint Director (T).

Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, age: 40 years, occupation: Teacher, r/o. Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, age: 40 years, occupation: Teacher, r/o. Sadasivpet.

### 3. Facts and Evidence:—

## a. Allegation:

the AO.

That Sri M. Janardhan, Jr. Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- on 9-2-2004 at his office, from the complainant Sri. Bodapally Ravi, s/o Shankarappa, age: 40 yrs. Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivpet. Medak District for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house ownership certificate.

Issuing

## b.  $ \underline{\text{Facts Leading to Registration of Case:}} $

Brief facts of the case are that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 AM the complainant Sri.B.Ravi, s/o Shankarappa, age:40yrs, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. The complainant stated that he has been working as a school assistant in Govt.

63

Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, is a retired teacher. They are having a house bearing No.3-4-30, at Yedla Bazar, in Ward No.11 of Sadashivpet.

Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office, Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate. As his father is old, he approached the concerned clerk Sri.Janardhan, in Sadashivpet Municipal office for house owner ship certificate. Then the AO.

Sri.Janardhan demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later when he met the AO.

Sri.Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him (complainant) whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe is paid.

Finally Sri.Janardhan stated that unless the bribe is paid, their application would not be processed.

He informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe.

Hence he came to ACB office Sangareddy on 6-2-2004, gave a report to the Inspector and requested to take necessary action against Sri.Janrdhan, Clerk, Municipal council, Sadashivpet, Medak district.

The Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, Medak District, Nizamabad Range caused discreet enquiries into the allegations and also about the genuineness of the compliant and came to know that Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District is awfully corrupt and enjoying bad reputation. Basing on the above complaint, the Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range, registered a case in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, u/s 7 of PC Act, 1988 on 9-2-2004 at 9.00 AM after observing all formalities and arrangements were made for laying a trap.

### 4. Pre Trap Actions :

During the course of investigation the Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy, secured the presence of two Govt. servants Viz., 1. Sri. Padma Narayana, s/o P. Ramaiah, age: 52 yrs. Occ: Asst. Gr-I, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, and 2. Sri. Tammalah Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Shivaiah, age: 46 yrs, Asst. Gr-III, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, and requested them to act as mediators in this case. Sri. P. Ramachandra Rao, Sri. Ch. Ravikanth, Inspectors, ACB, Nizamabad Range, and Sri. V. Vijaya Bhaskar, Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, were present. The Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, called the complainant and introduced him to the mediators and vice versa. The mediators have gone through the copy of the complaint dt. 6-2-2004 given by Sri. B. Ravi, Complainant and got verified the contents with him and found it to be correct and initialled on the same.

The Compliment

When the Inspector, ACB, asked the complainant about the bribe amount, he had produced Rs.1000/- (100 X 10 = 1000/-) which he intended to offer as bribe to Sri Janardhan, clerk of Municipal Council, Gadashivpet as per his demand at his office. On the instructions of the Inspector, ACB, one of the mediators collected the amount from the complainant, counted the currency notes and noted

d tiy dto.com

down the numbers and denominator. On the instruction of the inspector, ACB, Sri.N.Manikya Reddy, Sri N. Hamikya Reddy

HC 831 applied a thin layer of phenolphthalein powder to the said currency notes. The inspector then

got prepared sodium carbonate solution in a glass tumbler and asked the H.C. who applied

phenolphthalein powder to the currency notes to dip his fingers in the colourless sodium carbonate

solution. When he did so, the colour less solution turned into pink colour. Thus the reaction of

phenolphthalein powder with sodium carbonate solution has been demonstrated and the significance

of the test was explained. The Inspector ACB, directed the above H.C. to keep the tainted currency

complainant did not carry anything else in the pocket. The Inspector, ACB also instructed the

complainant to pay the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- to Sri.Janardhan, clerk in Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, only on his demand but not otherwise. He was also instructed to give signal by coming

out of the building to the trap party by wiping his face with his hand kerchief, in case the clerk

demands and accepts the bribe amount from him. The complainant was further instructed not to

shake hands with the Clerk before or after payment of bribe amount and not to touch the tainted

amount till it is paid to the clerk on his demand. The samples of phenoiphthaiein and sodium

carbonate powders which were used in the demonstration were preserved in two separate covers,

duly sealed and attested by the mediators and the Inspector.

The Inspector, ACB instructed Sri.Manikyareddy, HC to deposit the phenolphthalein powder box in the store room and stay back in the office. The mediators, complainant and ACB officials washed their hands with soap and water before leaving for trap to ensure that their hands are free from phenolphthalein powder. A mediators' report was drafted in the office room of Inspector, ACB. Medak at Sangareddy on 9-2-2004 from 9.30 A.M. to 10.30 AM by one of the mediators and it was attested by the other mediator and Inspectors of ACB Nizamabad Range

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//3378dacc-c195-48f1-99c0-1abc772c5b8d/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_904_3753_1061_3862.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A13Z%2F-1%2F%2F0e336d97aa98a2c97dd68082140f7fe69affc65f41d6468193e558e4c8dcea8a" alt="Image" width="4%" /></div>


5. Sequence of Trap happenings, recording of proceedings etc.

(1)

After completing the mediators' report – 1 in the office room of Inspector, ACB Medak district at Sangareddy, the complainant, mediators and ACB officials left the ACB office at 11.00 AM by an Ambassador car bearing No. AP-25U459 and two motor cycles bearing Nos. 1) AP-9-Q-2487, 2) AP-23-E-8154 and reached Sadashivpet town at about 12.00 Noon. After parking the vehicles on the road-side near Govt. Ravinder Model High School, Sadashivpet, the complainant Sri.B.Ravi was instructed to go to the clerk, Municipal Council. Sadashivpet with the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- and pay the amount to Sri.Janardhan, Clerk, only on his demand, but not otherwise. The entire trap party members followed the complainant and took vantage positions near the Municipal Office building and

67

waiting for the pre-arranged signal from the complainant. The complainant was allowed to go to the Municipal office building to comply with the demand of bribe by Sri Janardhan, Clerk in his office. He was seen entering into the building by the trap party at about 12-10 F.M.

At about 12-20 P.M. the complainant Sri.B.Ravi, came out of the Municipal Council office building and gave the pre arranged signal by wiping his face with hand kerchief indicating the demand and acceptance of the bribe amount of Rs.1000/- by Sri.Janardhan, Clerk. On receipt of the signal, the ACB officials and mediators rushed into the Municipal office building, One person was found sitting in a chair in the south-east corner of the staff room of Municipal Office. The ACB officials disclosed their identity, introduced the mediators and ascertained the name and address of that person as Mamidi Janardhan, s/o Narsimulloo, age:50 yrs, Occ: Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, R/o D.No.6-3-34/1, Raghavendra Nagar, Sadashivpet, Medak District. The complainant was asked to wait outside for some time. Then the Inspector, ACB, questioned Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, just then whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person. On that carbonate solution and requested Sri.M.Janardhan to rinse his both hand fingers separately in the said sodium carbonate solution. When he did so, the colourless solution is the right hand fingers turned into pink colour and the solutions in which he rinsed his left hand fingers remained colourless. When the ACB Inspector asked Sri.M.Janardhan to produce the tainted currency which he just then demanded and accepted from the complainant, Sri.M.Janardhan stated that the money is with one Ravi. When searched the pant pocket of Sri.M.Janardhan by the ACB officials a bunch of 100 rupee currency notes were found in his wearing pant right side pocket. On verification of the above currency notes by one of the mediators Sri.P.Narayana, it was found to be Rs.1000/- (One thousand) in 100 Rupee denomination. The numbers of the currency notes were tallied with the numbers of the currency notes mentioned in the mediators' report - I drafted at the O/o Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy on 9-2-04. The above amount of Rs.1000/- was seized by the inspector ACB. Sodium carbonate solution was prepared in another glass tumbler. The ACB inspector secured a new lungi through his subordinate and requested Sri.M.Janardhan to hand over his wearing pant by cladding the lungi. Accordingly Sri.M.Janardhan handover his wearing pant, and the inner flaps of the right side pocket of the pant was subjected to phenolphthalein test and it yielded positive result. The resultant solutions of the above three tests were transferred into three separate bottles, duly sealed and attested by the mediators. The wearing pant' Vimai made thick brown in colour of Sri.M.Janardhan was also seized by the ACB Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

69

 $$ \pi e A O $$ 

When the ACB Inspector questioned Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, about the demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house owner ship certificate, Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of Rs.1000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily. On demand by the Inspector, ACB, Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, produced the application given by the father of complainant and also other related documents. On perusal of the application, it was found that a note was written in Telugu for the approval and it was also found "please collect the fee and issue the certificate" in green ink with signature and date 9-2-2004. It was also noticed that the applicant has already paid Rs.100/- vide RB No.26/22516, dt.7-2-2004 and it was approved. Further the application dated:1-1-2004 of B.Shankarappa of Sadashivpet which was marked to "A1" Section vide inward registration No.324/2-1-04, was found enclosed to the note file along with copy of receipt of property tax paid by Bodapally Bakappa vide. Receipt No.012842, copy of certificate Nos A1/MIS/96, dt.20-4-96 issued by the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, duly certifying the house bearing No.3-4-30, stands in the name Bodapally Bakkappa and original certificate in Lr.No.A1/324/2004, dt.7-2-2004 issued by the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet on 9-2-2004 duly certified that the house bearing Municipal No.3-4-30 stands in the name of Sri.B.Bakkappa addressed to Sri.B.Shankarappa, s/o Bakappa, r/o Sadashivpet. The note file and enclosed documents containing sheet No.1 to 5 were seized by the ACB Inspector, duly attested by the mediators. Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, also produced miscellaneous receipt book. On perusal it was found the book is being maintained in book No.MF No.5% dt. Nil. The book containing receipts Nos.22501 to 22600 in duplicate. The receipt No.22516 was written in the name of Sri.B.Shankarappa, s/o Bakkappa, duly acknowledging receipt of Rs.100/- towards issue of owner-ship certificate duly signed by concerned clerk on behalf of the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet. The miscellaneous receipt book was seized by the inspector, ACB duly attested by the mediators.

Then the Inspector called the complainant Sri.B. Ravi and asked him to narrate as to what had all happened, after he left the trap party to go to the Jr. Asst. to meet his demand of bribe. Sri.B. Ravi stated that he went to the seat of Jr. Asst. in the office and found Sri. Janardhan, in his chair. He wished him and enquired about his work. Then Sri. Janardhan enquired whether he brought the demanded bribe amount of Rs.1000/-, He replied positively. Then Sri. Janardhan took out his application with related documents, after writing 3 or 4 lines in a paper, went to the chambers of the Municipal Commissioner and returned back within two or three minutes. Later Sri. M. Janardhan took him nearer to bath room adjacent to the office, and demanded him to pay the bribe amount of

71

Rs.1000/-. Then he took out the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- from his shirt pocket and paid to

Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst who received the amount with his right hand, and kept it in his wearing

pant right side pocket. Then Sri.Janardhan and himself came back to his seat and Janardhan-issued

him the house ownership certificate dt.9-2-2004 duly signed by the Commissioner. So saying the

complaint produced the certificate. On perusal of the certificate it was noticed that the certificate was

issued by the Commissioner on 9-2-2004, counter-signed by Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, on 9-2-2004 in

Lr.No.A1/324/2004, dt.7-2-2004 duly certifying that the house bearing Municipal No.3-4-30 stands in

the name of Bakkappa, Sadashivpet. As the office copy of the certificate was already seized along

with note file, the certificate was returned to Sri.B.Ravi to avoid inconvenience.

The ACB Inspector examined the Municipal Commissioner who was found at his chamber at the time of trap. On examination Sri.R.Kistaiah, s/o Balaiah, age:53 yrs, Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, stated that he has been working as Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet since 5-2-2004. Sri.M.Janardhan, has been working as Jr.Asst. in his office. On 9-2-2004 at about 12.15 PM while he was in his office chamber, Sri.M.Janardhan, came to him for approval of a note file pertaining to issue of house ownership certificate to one B.Shankarappa. On the note file he has written to collect the fee and issue certificate. Later within five minutes M.Janardhan came with the note file stating that the fee of Rs.100/- was already paid by the applicant on 7-2-2004. Immediately he approved the note file and signed the certificate in duplicate and put date 9-2-2004. Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, used to deal with subjects such as issue of ownership certificates, mutation certificates, assessment and collection of revenue. For issue of house owner-ship certificate, the applicant has to give an application in writing duly enclosing a copy of house tax paid receipt. After receiving the application the Commissioner will mark the same to the concerned section after entering in inward register. The concerned clerk after receiving the application will put up a note file after verification of office records for his approval and the certificate will be issued if the applicant has no dues of taxes. As per the existing rules house ownership certificate has to be issued within 5 days from the date of receipt of application from the applicant. In the instant case the applicant Sri.B.Shankarappa has submitted his application on 2-1-04 vide inward register entry No.324, dated.02-01-2004. The application was said to be given to Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, for verification and put up on 4-1-2004. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, without valid reasons. The statement of the Commissioner was recorded by the Inspector, ACB.

73

On the request of the ACB Inspector, the Commissioner produced the Attendance Register of his staff. On perusal it was noticed that the register is being maintained from Jan-2003 to 9-2-2004. On the ACO

9-2-2004 Sri.M. Janardhan signed in the Attendance Register. The Attendance Register was seized by the Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

Another Junior Asst. was also found in a chair in the south west corner of the staff room. On examination, the Junior Assistant Sri.B.Satyanarayana s/o B.Laxmaiah age, 56 yrs, r/o Sadashivpet stated that he has been working as Junior Asst. since 14-11-2003. He used to deal with inward/outward sections and issue of Birth and Death certificates. On 2-1-2004 he received an application from one B.Ravi of Sadashivpet duly endorsed by the then commissioner. He entered the particulars in inward register vide SI.No.324. As the concerned Clerk M. Janardhan was not available in his seat on 2-1-2004, he has handed over the application to Sri.M. Janardhan on 4-1-2004 for further action at his end. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with Sri.M. Janardhan. He further added that he has not witnessed the transaction that took place between the AO Sri.M. Janardhan, and Sri.B.Ravi. When Sri.M. Janardhan was shouting after the arrival of ACB officials he came to know about the trap. The statement of Sri.B.Satyanarayana, Jr. Asst., was recorded by the ACB officials. On the request of the ACB Inspector Sri.B.Satyanarayana Jr. Asst. has produced the inward register from his custody. On perusal of the register, it was noticed that the register was maintained from 25-10-2002 to 3-2-2004. In page No.153 of the register, the application particulars in the instant case (B.Shankarappa, H.no.3-4-304, owner ship certificate A1) were entered vide. SI.No.324. The inward register containing page Nos.1 to 170 was seized by the ACB Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

 $$ \because\angle1=45^{\circ},A O\cdot $$ 

The Inspector, ACB confronted the Jr. Asst, Sri. M. Janardhan, with the version of complainant and the records about the demand and acceptance of bribe from the complainant. Sri. M. Janardhan, reiterated his earlier statement that he has not demanded any bribe from the complainant but accepted an amount of Rs. 1000/- from Sri. B. Ravi when he offered the amount voluntarily.

A rough sketch of the scene of offence i.e. Municipal office building Sadashivpet was drawn showing the seat of the Jr. Asst. and position of the trap party and mediators out side the building and it was attested by the mediators. The sample of sodium carbonate powder used for preparation of solutions was preserved in a cover, duly sealed and attested by the mediators.

Jr. Asst, O/o Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District was arrested by the Inspector, ACB at 4.10 PM on 9.2.2004 after informing the grounds of his arrest.

A detailed proceedings (Mediators Report-II) was drafted in the staff room of O/O Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District by one of the mediators on 9-2-2004 from 12.40 PM to 4.15 PM by incorporating the above facts and it was attested by the other mediator.

75

and inspectors of ACB, Nizamabad Range. A copy of the mediators' report-ii was supplied to the accused officer under proper acknowledgement.

### 6. Post Trap Actions:

The Inspectors ACB, Nizamabad Range searched the house of A.O. situated at D.No.6-3-34/1, Raghavendra Nagar, Sadashivpet, Medak District in the presence of Mediators on 9-2-2004 and found no incriminating material or worth while assets. The A.O. Sri M. Janardhan, Jr. Asst. was produced before the Hon'ble Judge for SPE & ACB cases, Hyderabad on 10-2-2004 for Judicial custody and he was released on bail on 11-2-2004. The statement of the complainant u/s 164 Cr.P.C. was recorded by the Hon'ble JFCM & Special Mobile Magistrate, Sangareddy on 21-2-2004.

### 7. Oral Evidence:

Janardhan was trapped by ACB officials when he demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs.1000/- from his son and etc., facts

10) REBUTTAL TO THE EXPLANATION OF THE A.O.:

The A.O. has taken wavering pleas. Immediately after the trap, when the Inspector questioned the A.O. whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person, the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand-fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation. When the A.O. did not demand any bribe from any person, there was no need to rub his hand-fingers to his pants. Apart from this, he did not give any explanation.

Again when the Inspector questioned the A.O. about demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1,000/ from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily. On demand by the Inspector, A.C.B., the A.O. produced the application given by the father of the complainant and also other related documents. On perusal of the application it was found that a note was written in Telugu for the approval and it was also found as written "please collect the fee and issue the certificate" in green ink with signature and date 9-2-2004. It was further noticed that the applicant already paid Rs.100/- vide RB No.26/22516, dated 7-2-2004 and it was approved.

EXPLANATION OF THE A.O.

Immediately after the trap, when the inspector questioned the A.O. whether he

demanded and accepted any bribe from any person just then, the A.O. became

nervous, tried to rub his hand-fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation.

Again when the Inspector questioned the A.O. about demand and acceptance of

bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the

complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate, the A.O. denied to have

demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of

Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily.

Further, when the Inspector confronted the A.O. with the version of the complainant about the demand and acceptance of bribe from the complainant, the A.O. reiterated his earlier statement that he did not demand any bribe from the complainant but accepted an amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant when he offered the amount voluntarily.

### 9. Explanation of the Accused Officer:

The accused officer Sri.M.Janardhan, when asked his explanation during trap proceedings denied to have demanded bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant. On 25-2-2004 the A.O submitted his written explanation wherein he contended that he was falsely implicated in this case. He further mentioned in his explanation that neither he demanded nor accepted any bribe from the complainant at any point of time. Finally he requested to drop all further proceedings against him in this case.

### 10. Rebuttal to the Explanation of the A.O.:]

The contention of the accused officer is totally false. On 4-1-2004 the A.O. received the application of the complainant for process and put up before the Commissioner. The work of the complainant had to be completed within 5 days from the date of receipt of the application. The Municipal Commissioner Sri.R.Kistalah, stated that the file of the complainant was pending with the A.O. from 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004. But the A.O. kept the file pending for more than a month for the reasons best known to him. On 9-2-2004 soon after receipt of the bribe amount the A.O. delivered

### 11. FINDINGS & RECOMMENDATIONS:

In view of the oral and documentary evidence, it is established beyond reasonable doubt that the A.O. Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant Sri B. Ravi, Sio. Sri Shankarappa, a Teacher of Sadasivpet, Nizamabad District on 9-2-2004 for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house ownership certificate.

It is, therefore, recommended that the A.O. may be prosecuted in a Court of Law u/Ss. 7 & 13(2), r/w. Sec. 13(1)(d) of the Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

Further, the A.O. acquired a house bearing No. 6-3-34/4/1M worth Rs.6

lakns in his name and a plot bearing No.6-3-10 worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of

his wife in Sadasivpet Municipality, besides a scooter bearing No. AP 23 7255 without

obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed to submit his annual

property statements from the date of his appointment to till 9-2-2004.

Hence, departmental action may be initiated against the A.O. for the lapses mentioned above.

The contention of the A.O. neither he demanded nor accepted any bribe from the complainant at any point of time is nothing but false. The A.O denied to process the application till the bribe is paid. As and when the complainant approached the A.O. for his work he told the complainant that the application will be processed as and when the bribe is paid and that unless the bribe is paid the application would not be processed. The same facts are evident from the statement of the complainant. The positive result of the phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers and pant pocket of the A.O. and recovery of the tainted amount from his possession is a conclusive proof that the A.O. has accepted the bribe. When questioned the A.O. during trap proceedings he admitted to have accepted the bribe from the complainant. Hence the contention of the A.O. is nothing but false.

Faith of FIR, mediation Report ---- (Slip attached)

11.

#### Findings and Recommendations:

The accused officer Sri. M. Janardhan, S/o Narsimulu, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, Medak Dist had demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant

Sri. B. Ravi s/o Shankarappa, age: 40 yrs, Occ: School Assistant, R/o Sadashivpet Town, Medak

District on 9-2-2004 for showing official favour in processing the application of complainant's father for

issue of house owner ship certificate. The complainant stated the same facts in his 161 Cr.P.C

statement. The phenolphthalein test conducted on right hand fingers and inner flaps of the pant

pocket of the A.O. yielded positive result. The tainted amount of Rs.1000/- was recovered from the

possession of the A.O. The pending file of the complainant was also seized from the possession of

the A.O.

Enquiries about the reputation of A.O. revealed that the A.O. is awfully corrupt and enjoying bad reputation.

Hence the allegation of demand and acceptance of bribe by the A.O. Sri.M. Janardhan, is held substantiated. There is ample evidence on record to prove the guilt of the A.O. in a court of law.

Hence it is a fit case to recommend prosecution of the A.O. in a Court of Law.

## C.No.50/RCT-NMD/2004.dt.18-5-2004

Sir.

Sub:- Public Servants --- Allegation of corruption -- Demand and acceptance of bribe by Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District --- Trapped on 9-2-2004 --- Final Report --- Sending of --- Reg.

Ref: - 1) This Office Preliminary Report of even number, dt. 17-2-2004.

2) V.C's Letter No.84/VC.D1/04-2, dated 28-2-2004.

## INTRODUCTION:

In continuation to this Office Preliminary Report 1 $ ^{st} $ cited, following is the Final Report in this case of trap, wherein Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs.1,000/- on 9-2-2004 at his Office from one Sri B. Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, age: 40 years, occupation: Teacher, r/o. Sadasivpet, Medak District for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing of house ownership certificate.

Sri Mamidi Janardhan Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet,

Medak District will hereinafter be referred to as Accused Officer (A.O.) and Sri B. Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, age: 40 years, occupation: Teacher, r/o. Sadasivpet,

Medak District will hereinafter be referred to as the complainant.

### 2. The Service particulars of the Accused Officer are as follows:

### 3. Facts and Evidence:

a.  $ \underline{\text{Allegation:}} $

That A.O. demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1,000/- on 9-2-2004 at his office, from the complainant for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate.

## b. Facts Leading to Registration of Case:

Brief facts of the case are that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 AM the complainant came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. The complainant stated that he has been working as a School Assistant in Govt. Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, is a retired Teacher. They own a house bearing No.3-4-30 at Yedla Bazar, in Ward No.11 of Sadashivpet Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office, Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate. As his father is old, he approached the AO on behalf of his father at Sadashivpet Municipal Council office for house owner-ship certificate. The A.O. demanded Rs.1,000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later again when he met A.O. for the certificate, he ascertained from him (complainant) whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally A.O. stated that unless the bribe is paid, the application would not be processed and he informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe. Hence he came to ACB office at Sangareddy on 6-2-2004, gave a report to the Inspector and requested to take necessary action against A.O.

The Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, Medak District, Nizamabad Range caused discreet enquiries into the allegations and also about the genuineness of the compliant and came to know that the A.O. is awfully corrupt and enjoying bad reputation. Basing on the above complaint, the Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range, registered a case in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, u/s 7 of PC Act, 1988 on 9-2-2004 at 9.00 AM after observing all formalities and arrangements were made for laying a trap.

### 4. Pre Trap Actions :-

During the course of investigation the Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy, secured the presence of two Govt. servants Viz., 1. Sri.Pandla Narayana, s/o Sri P.Ramaiah, age: 52 yrs. Occ: Asst. Gr-I, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, and 2. Sri.Tammala Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Sri Shivaiah, age: 46 yrs, Asst. Gr-III, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, and requested them to act as mediators in this case. Sri.P.Ramachandra Rao, Sri.Ch.Ravikanth, Inspectors, ACB, Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad and Sri.V. Vijaya Bhaskar, Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, were present. The Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, called the complainant and introduced him to the mediators and vice versa. The mediators have gone through the copy of the complaint dt.6-2-2004 given by the Complainant, verified the contents with him, found it to be correct and initialled on the same.

The complainant produced Rs.1000/- (100 X 10 = 1000/-) which he intended to offer as bribe to AO as per his demand at his office. On the instructions of the Inspector, ACB, one of the mediators collected the amount from the complainant, counted the currency notes and noted down their numbers and denominations. On the instructions of the Inspector, ACB, HC 831 Sri N.Manikya Reddy applied a thin layer of phenolphthalein powder to the said currency notes. The Inspector then got prepared sodium carbonate solution prepared in a glass tumbler and asked the H.C. who applied phenolphthalein powder to the currency notes, to rinse his fingers in the colourless sodium carbonate solution. When he did so, the colourless solution turned into pink colour. Thus the reaction of phenolphthalein powder with sodium carbonate solution was demonstrated and the significance of the test was explained. The Inspector ACB, directed the H.C. to keep the tainted currency notes of Rs.1000/- in the left side emptied pocket of complainant's shirt. The Inspector, ACB, instructed the complainant not to touch the tainted amount till it is paid to the AO on his demand and also instructed the

complainant to pay the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- to A.O. only on his demand but not other-wise. He was also instructed to give signal by coming out of the building to the trap party by wiping his face with his hand kerchief, in case the A.O. demands and accepts the bribe amount from him. The complainant was further instructed not to shake hands with the A.O. before or after payment of bribe amount and not to touch the tainted amount till it was paid to the A.O. on his demand.

The samples of phenolphthalein and sodium carbonate powders which were used in the demonstration were preserved in two separate covers, duly sealed and attested by the mediators and the Inspector.

The Inspector, ACB instructed Sri.Manikyareddy, HC to deposit the phenolphthalein powder box in the store room and stay back in the office. The mediators, the complainant and the ACB officials washed their hands with soap and water before leaving for trap to ensure that their hands are free from phenolphthalein powder.

A mediators' report was drafted in the office room of Inspector, ACB Medak District at Sangareddy on 9-2-2004 from 9.30 A.M. to 10.30 AM by one of the mediators and it was attested by the other mediator and Inspectors of ACB Nizamabad Range.

### 5. Sequence of Trap and recording of proceedings etc.

After completing the mediators' report – I in the office room of Inspector, 'ACB Medak district at Sangareddy, the complainant, the mediators and the ACB officials left the ACB office at 11.00 AM by an Ambassador car bearing No. AP-25U459 and two motor cycles bearing Nos. 1) AP-9-Q-2487, 2) AP-23-E-8154 and reached Sadashivpet town at about 12.00 Noon. After parking the vehicles on the road side near Govt. Ravinder Model High School, Sadashivpet, the complainant was instructed to go to the A.O. with the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- and pay the amount to him, only on his demand, but not otherwise. The entire trap party members followed the complainant and took vantage positions near the Municipal Office building and waited for the prearranged signal from the complainant. The complainant was allowed to go to the Municipal office building to comply with the demand of bribe by the A.O. in his office. He was seen entering into the building by the trap party at about 12-10 P.M.

At about 12-20 P.M. the complainant came out of the Municipal Council office building and gave the pre arranged signal by wiping his face with hand kerchief indicating the demand and acceptance of the bribe amount of Rs.1000/- by the A.O. On receipt of the signal, the ACB officials and the mediators rushed into the Municipal office building and found one person sitting in a chair in the south east corner of the staff room of Municipal Office. The ACB officials disclosed their identity, to the said person, introduced the mediators to him and ascertained the name and address of that person as Mamidi Janardhan s/o Sri Narsimuloo, (AO) age:50 yrs, Occ: Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, R/o D.No.6-3-34/1, Raghavendra Nagar, Sadashivpet, Medak District. The complainant was asked to wait outside for some time. Then the Inspector, ACB, questioned the A.O. just then whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person. On that the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation. Then the Inspector, ACB secured water in two glasses and got prepared sodium carbonate solutions prepared and requested the A.O. to rinse his both hand fingers separately in the said sodium carbonate solutions. When he did so, the colourless solutions in which he rinsed his right hand fingers turned into pink colour and the solutions in which he rinsed his left hand fingers remained colourless. When the ACB Inspector asked the A.O. to produce the tainted currency which he just then demanded and accepted from the complainant, The A.O. stated that the money was with one Ravi. When ACB officials searched the pants pocket of the A.O., a bunch of 100/rupee currency notes was found in his wearing pants right side pocket. On verification of the currency notes by one of the mediators Sri.P.Narayana, they were found to be Rs.1000/- (One thousand) in 100/- Rupee denominations. The numbers of the currency notes tallied with the numbers of the currency notes mentioned in the mediators report - I drafted at the O/O Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy on 9-2-04. The above amount of Rs.1000/- was seized by the Inspector ACB. Sodium carbonate solution was prepared in another glass tumbler. The ACB Inspector secured a new lungi through his subordinate and requested the A.O. to hand over his wearing pants by cladding the lungi. Accordingly, the A.O. handed over his wearing pants, and when the inner flap of the right side pocket of A.O.'s pant was subjected to phenolphthalein test, and it yielded positive result. The resultant solutions of the above three tests were transferred into three separate bottles, duly sealed and attested by the mediators. The wearing pants, "Vimal" made thick brown in colour of the A.O. was also seized by the ACB Inspector duly attested by the mediator.

When the ACB Inspector questioned the A.O., about the demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house owner-ship certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily. On demand by the Inspector, ACB, the A.O. produced the application given by the father of complainant and also other related documents. On perusal of the application, it was found that a note was written in Telugu for the approval and it was also found "please collect the fee and issue the certificate" in green ink with signature and date 9-2-2004. It was also noticed that the applicant already paid Rs.100/- vide RB No.26/22516, dt.7-2-2004 and it was approved. Further the application dated:1-1-2004 of c SRI B.Shankarappa of Sadashivpet which was marked to "A1" Section vide inward registration No.324/2-1-04, was found enclosed to the note file along with copy of receipt of property tax paid by one Sri Bodapally Bakappa vide. Receipt No.012842, copy of certificate Nos A1/MISC/96, dt.20-4-96 issued by the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet Municipality, duly certifying the house bearing No.3-4-30, stands in the name of Sri Bodapally Bakappa and original certificate in Lr.No.A1/324/2004, dt.7-2-2004 issued by Commissioner Municipal Council, Sadashivpet on 9-2-2004 duly certified that the house bearing Municipal No.3-4-30 stands in the name of Sri.B.Bakappa addressed to Sri.B.Shankarappa, s/o Bakkappa, r/o Sadashivpet. The note file and enclosed documents containing sheet No 1 to 5 were seized by the ACB Inspector, duly attested by the mediators. The A.O. produced miscellaneous receipt book also. On perusal it was found the book is being maintained in book No.MF No.52, dt. Nil. The book containing receipts Nos.22501 to 22600 in duplicate upto Receipt No.2516 were written. The receipt No.22516 was written in the name of Sri.B.Shankarappa, s/o Sri Bakkappa, duly acknowledging receipt of Rs.100/- towards issue of owner ship certificate duly signed by concerned clerk on behalf of the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet. The miscellaneous receipt book was seized by the Inspector, ACB duly attested by the mediators.

Then the Inspector called the complainant and asked him to narrate as to what had all happened, after he left the trap party to go to the A.O. to meet his demand of bribe. The complainant stated that he went to the seat of the A.O. in the office and found him in his chair. He wished him and enquired about his work. Then the A.O. enquired whether he brought the demanded bribe amount of Rs.1,000/-, on which he

replied positively. Then the A.O. took out his application with related documents, after writing 3 or 4 lines in a paper, went to the chambers of the Municipal Commissioner and returned within two or three minutes. Later the A.O., took him nearer to bath room adjacent to the office, and demanded him to pay the bribe amount of Rs.1,000/-. Then he took out the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- from his shirt pocket and paid to the A.O. who received the amount with his right hand, and kept it in his wearing pant right side pocket. Then the A.O. and himself came back to his seat and the A.O. issued him the house ownership certificate dt.9-2-2004 duly signed by the Commissioner. So saying the complaint produced the certificate. On perusal of the certificate, it was noticed that the certificate was issued by the Commissioner on 9-2-2004, counter signed by the A.O. on 9-2-2004 in Lr.No.A1/324/2004, dt.7-2-2004 duly certifying that the house bearing Municipal No.3-4-30 stands in the name of Sri Bakkappa, Sadashivpet. As the office copy of the certificate was already seized along with note file, the certificate was returned to the complainant to avoid inconvenience.

The ACB Inspector examined the Municipal Commissioner who was found at his chamber at the time of trap. On examination Sri.R.Kistaiah, s/o Sri Balaiah, age:53 yrs, Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, stated that he has been working as Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet since 5-2-2004. The A.O. has been working as Jr.Asst. in his office. On 9-2-2004 at about 12.15 PM while he was in his office chamber, the A.O., came to him for approval of a note file pertaining to issue of house ownership certificate to one Sri B.Shankarappa. On the note file he wrote to collect the fee and issue certificate. Later within five minutes the A.O. came with the note file stating that the fee of Rs.100/- was already paid by the applicant on 7-2-2004. Immediately he approved the note file and signed the certificate in duplicate and put date 9-2-2004. The A.O. used to deal with subjects such as issue of ownership certificates, mutation certificates, assessment and collection of revenue. For issue of house owner-ship certificate, the applicant has to give an application in writing duly enclosing a copy of house tax paid receipt. After receiving the application the Commissioner marks the same to the concerned section after entering in inward register. The clerk concerned after receiving the application, puts up a note file after verification of office records for his approval and the certificate is issued if the applicant has no dues of taxes. As per the existing rules, house ownership certificate has to be issued within 5 days from the date of receipt of application from the applicant. In the instant case, the applicant Sri.B.Shankarappa submitted his application on 2-1-04 vide

inward register entry No.324, dated.02-01-2004. The application was said to be given to the A.O. for verification and put up on 4-1-2004. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with the A.O. without any valid reasons. The statement of the Commissioner was recorded by the Inspector, ACB.

On the request of the ACB Inspector, the Commissioner produced the Attendance Register of his staff. On perusal it was noticed that the register was maintained from Jan-2003 to 9-2-2004. On 9-2-2004 the A.O. signed in the Attendance Register. The Attendance Register was seized by the Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

Another Junior Asst. was also found in a chair in the south west corner of the staff room. On examination, the Junior Assistant Sri.B.Satyanarayana s/o Sri B.Laxmaiah age, 56 yrs, r/o Sadashivpet stated that he has been working as Junior Asst. since 14-11-2003. He used to deal with inward/outward sections and issue of Birth and Death certificates. On 2-1-2004 he received an application from one B.Ravi of J - Sadashivpet duly endorsed by the then Commissioner. He entered the particulars in inward register vide SI.No.324. As the clerk concerned, the A.O. was not available in his seat on 2-1-2004, he handed over the application to him on 4-1-2004 for further action at his end. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with the J-A.O. He further added that he did not witnessed the transaction that took place between the A.O. and the complainant. When the A.O. was shouting after the arrival of ACB officials he came to know about the trap. The statement of Sri.B.Satyanarayana, Jr. Asst., was recorded by the ACB officials. On the request of the ACB Inspector Sri.B.Satyanarayana Jr. Asst. produced the inward register from his custody. On perusal of the register, it was noticed that the register was maintained from 25-10-2002 to 3-2-2004. In page No.153 of the register, the application particulars in the instant case (B.Shankarappa, H.no.3-4-304, owner ship certificate A1) were entered vide. SI.No.324. The inward register containing page Nos.1 to 170 was seized by the ACB Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

The Inspector, ACB confronted the A.O. with the version of complainant and the records about the demand and acceptance of bribe from the complainant. The A.O. reiterated his earlier statement that he did not demand any bribe from the complainant.

##### t

but accepted an amount of Rs.1000/- from the complainant when he offered the amount voluntarily.

A rough sketch of the scene of offence i.e. Municipal office building Sadashivpet was drawn showing the seat of the A.O. and position of the trap party and mediators out side the building and it was attested by the mediators. The sample of sodium carbonate powder used for preparation of solutions was preserved in a cover, duly sealed and attested by the mediators.

The A.O. was arrested by the Inspector, ACB at 4.10 PM on 9.2.2004 after informing the grounds of his arrest.

A detailed proceedings (Mediators Report-II) was drafted in the staff room of O/o Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District by one of the mediators on 9-2-2004 from 12.40 PM to 4.15 PM by incorporating the above facts and it was attested by the other mediator, and Inspectors of ACB, Nizamabad Range. A copy of the mediators' report-II was supplied to the accused officer under proper acknowledgement.

### 6. Post Trap Actions:

The Inspectors ACB, Nizamabad Range searched the house of A.O. situated at D.No.6-3-34/1, Raghavendra Nagar, Sadashivpet, Medak District in the presence of Mediators on 9-2-2004 and found no incriminating material or worth while assets.

The A.O. was produced before the Hon'ble Judge for SPE & ACB cases,

Hyderabad on 10-2-2004 for Judicial custody and he was released on bail on

11-2-2004.

The statement of the complainant u/s 164 Cr.P.C. was recorded by the Hon'ble JFCM & Special Mobile Magistrate, Sangareddy on 21-2-2004.

### 7. Oral Evidence:

During the course of investigation the following witnesses were examined and their statements were recorded.

1. Statement of Sri.Bodapally Ravi, s/o Sri Shanakarappa, age: 40 yrs, occ: School Assistant r/o D.No.3-4-30, Sadashivpet, Medak district.

He is the complainant in this case. His statement u/s 161Cr.P.C. was recorded in detail. The complainant corroborated the contents of the FIR and narrated the pre-trap and post trap events in sequence. He also fared well in his 164 Cr.P.C. statement also recorded by the JFCM and Special Mobile Magistrate, Sangareddy.

### 2. Statement of Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, S/o Sri Bakkappa, age: 68 yrs, Occ: Retd Teacher R/o D.No.3-4-30, Sadashivpet, Medak district.

He is the father of the complainant. He stated that he had a house bearing No. 3-4-30 in Sadashivpet town. The house is in the name of his father Sri Bakkappa. He is the only legal heir to the property. On 2-1-2004 he along with his son Ravi (complainant) went to Municipal Office, Sadashivpet and gave an application to the Commissioner for issue of house owner ship certificate. The then Municipal Commissioner endorsed the application to A1 section and directed them to hand over the application in inward section. Accordingly on the same day they handed over the application to the Jr. Asst. at inward section. Later on, his son (complainant) approached the Municipal office for the certificate. He further stated that his son informed him that the concerned clerk in the office was demanding Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his application. But he told his son not to pay the bribe to the concerned clerk and report the matter to the ACB officials. On 9-2-2004 evening he came to know that the A.O. was trapped by ACB officials when he demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs.1000/- from his son and etc., facts

### 3. Statement of Sri.R.Kistaiah, s/o Sri Balaiah, age:53 yrs, Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak Dist.

He stated that he has been working as Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet since 5-2-2004. The A.O. has been working as Jr.Asst. in his office. On 9-2-2004 at about 12.15 PM while he was in his office chamber, the A.O. came to him for approval of a note file pertaining to issue of house ownership certificate to one Sri B.Shankarappa. On the note file he has wrote to collect the fee and issue certificate. Later within five minutes, the A.O. came with the note file stating that the fee of Rs.100/- was already paid by the applicant on 7-2-2004. Immediately he approved the note file and signed the certificate in duplicate and put date 9-2-2004. The A.O.

used to deal with subjects such as issue of owner ship certificate, mutation certificate, assessment and collection of revenue. For issue of house owner ship certificate, the applicant has to give an application in writing duly enclosing a copy of house tax paid receipt. After receiving the application, the Commissioner marks the same to the concerned section after entering in inward register. The concerned clerk, after receiving the application puts up a note file after verification of office records for his approval and the certificate is issued if the applicant has no dues of taxes. As per the existing rules, house ownership certificate has to be issued within 5 days from the date of receipt of application from the applicant. In the instant case, the applicant Sri.B.Shankarappa submitted his application on 2-1-04 vide inward register entry No.324, dated.02-01-2004. The application was said to be given to the A.O., on 4-1-2004 for verification and put up. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with the A.O. without any valid reasons and etc., facts.

### 4. Statement of Sri.B.Satyanarayana s/o Sri B.Laxmaiah age,56yrs, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District.

He stated that he has been working as Junior Asst., in Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, since 14-11-2003. He used to deal with inward/outward sections and

issue of Birth and Death certificates. On 2-1-2004 he received an application from one

B. Ravi of Sadashivpet duly endorsed by the then Commissioner. He entered the

particulars in inward register vide SI. No. 324. As the concerned clerk (A1) the A.O. was

not available in his seat on 2-1-2004 he has handed over the application to him on

4-1-2004 for further action at his end. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004, the application was

pending with the A.O. He further added that he did not witnessed the transaction that

took place between the A.O. and the complainant. When the A.O. was shouting after

the arrival of ACB officials, he came to know about the trap. On the request of the ACB

Inspector, he handed over the inward register from his custody and etc, facts.

5. Statement of Smt.Sheelam Pankaja, w/o Sri D.V.S.Sastri, age:27yrs, occ:Valuation Officer, circle-II, Municipal corporation, Hyderabad city, R/o 1-2-186, Shanthinagar, Adilabad District.

She stated that she has been working as valuation officer in Municipal Corporation, Hyderabad City since 28-1-2004. Previously she worked as Municipal Commissioner, Sadashivpet Municipality, Medak district from 9-7-2001 to 27-1-2004. The A.O., has worked in the Sadashivpet Municipality during her tenure. He used to

look after the revenue section and process the applications pertaining to mutation, property owner ship certificates, property assessments, etc., On 2-1-2004, she received an application from one Sri B.Shankarappa of Sadashivpet for issue of house owner-ship certificate and endorsed the same to A1 section which was being looked after by the A.O. From 3-1-2004 to 23-1-2004 she was on leave. On 24-1-2004 she reported for duty and relieved from the Municipality on 27-1-2004. But the A.O. did not put up the note file for issue of house owner ship certificate to Sri B.Shankarappa and etc, facts.

6. Sri.D.Narsinga Rao, Judicial First Class Magistrate, Sangareddy, Medak District, Special Mobile Magistrate.

He recorded the statement of the complainant u/s 164 Cr.P.C. on 21-2-2004.

7. Sri. Pandla Narayana S/0 Sri P.Ramaiah, Age:52 Yrs, Assistant Gr.I, O/o District Manager, Civil Supplies Department, Sangareddy.

He is one of the mediators and will speak the facts of mediators report-I and mediators report-II in this case.

8. Sri.T.Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Sri Shiavaiah, age:46 yrs, Assistant Gr.III, O/o District Manager, Civil Supplies Department, Sangareddy.

He is another mediator and will speak the facts of mediators report-I and mediators report-II in this case.

8 The inward register containing page Nos.1 to 170, wherein the particulars of the application submitted by the complainant's father were entered in Page. No.153 vide SI.No.324, dt.2-1-2004.

9. The Attendance Register of the staff of Commissioner, Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, Medak District. It shows the presence of A.O. in the office on the

date of trap.

10. Copy of 164 Cr.P.C. Statement of complainant.

11. Letter in ROC No.C1/MCS/2004, Dated:9-3-2004 of the commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet Medak district. The Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet informed through this letter that the A.O. has not submitted his annual property statements to his higher authorities from date of his appointment to till 9-3-2004 and but acquired a house bearing No.6-3-34/4/1M, worth about Rs.6 lakhs in his name of A.O., one plot No.6-3-10 200 Sq yards worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of Smt.M.Suguna, wife of A.O. in Sadashivapet municipality, besides a scooter bearing No.AP23 7255.

### 9.  $ \underline{\text{Explanation of the Accused Officer}} $:

Immediately after the trap, when the Inspector questioned the A.O. whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person just then, the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand-fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation.

Again when the Inspector questioned the A.O. about demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily.

On 25-2-2004 the A.O submitted his written explanation wherein he contended that he was falsely implicated in this case. He further mentioned in his explanation that neither he demanded nor accepted any bribe from the complainant at any point of time. Finally he requested to drop all further proceedings against him in this case.

### 10. Rebuttal to the Explanation of the A.O.:

The A.O. has taken wavering pleas. Immediately after the trap, when the Inspector questioned the A.O. whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person, the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand-fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation.

Again when the Inspector questioned the A.O. about demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily. On demand by the Inspector, A.C.B., the A.O. produced the application given by the father of the complainant and also other related documents. On perusal of the application it was found that a note was written in Telugu for the approval and it was also found as written "please collect the fee and issue the certificate" in green ink with signature dated 9-2-2004. It was further noticed that the applicant already paid Rs.100/- vide RB No.26/22516, dated 7-2-2004 and it was approved.

On 4-1-2004 the A.O. received the application of the complainant for process

As P. The standing had to be completed

and put up to the Commissioner. The work of the complainant had to be completed

within 5 days from the date of receipt of the application. The Municipal Commissioner

Sri. R. Kistaiah, stated that the file of the complainant was pending with the A.O. from 4-

1-2004 to 9-2-2004. But the A.O. kept the file pending for more than a month with an

intention to extract bribe from the complainant. On 9-2-2004 soon after receipt of the

bribe amount, the A.O. delivered the house ownership certificate to the complainant.

Prior to this step, the complainant has to pay

A.O. but the client determined the罚款是否符合合同约定。

The contention of the A.O. in his written explanation that neither he demanded

nor accepted any bribe from the complainant at any point of time is nothing but false.

# of fall in

The A.O refused to process the application till the bribe was paid. Whenever the complainant approached the A.O. for his work he told the complainant that the application will be processed as and when the bribe was paid and that unless the bribe is paid, the application would not be processed. and on this day, I had after coming to the am 非完

Facts of F.I.R., Mediators' Reports I & II, recovery of the tainted amount from the A.O.'s pant pocket, positive chemical tests on the right hand-fingers of the A.O. and the inner-flap of his pant pocket, and the pending file with the A.O. at the time of trap are all conclusive proof that the A.O. demanded and accepted Rs.1,000/- as bribe from the complainant.

### 11. FINDINGS & RECOMMENDATIONS:

In view of the oral and documentary evidence, it is established beyond reasonable doubt that the A.O. Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant Sri B. Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, a Teacher of Sadasivpet, Nizamabad District on 9-2-2004 for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house ownership certificate.

It is, therefore, recommended that the A.O. may be prosecuted in a Court of Law u/Ss. 7 & (13(2), r/w. Sec. 13(1)(d) of the Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

13(1)(d) v1ω13(2)

unquaint veiled

Further, the A.O. acquired a house bearing No. 6-3-34/4/1M worth Rs.6 lakhs in his name and a plot bearing No.6-3-10 worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of his wife in Sadasivpet Municipality, besides a scooter bearing No. AP 23 7255 without obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed to submit his annual property statements from the date of his appointment to till 9-2-2004.

Hence, departmental action may be initiated against the A.O. for the lapses mentioned above.

Sub:- Public Servants --- Allegation of corruption -- Demand and acceptance of bribe by Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District --- Trapped on 9-2-2004 --- Final Report --- Sending of --- Reg.

Ref: -1) This Office Preliminary Report of even number, dt. 17-2-2004.

2) V.C's Letter No.84/VC.D1/04-2, dated 28-2-2004.

## INTRODUCTION:

In continuation to this Office Preliminary Report 1 $ ^{st} $ cited, following is the Final Report in this case of trap, wherein Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs.1,000/- on 9-2-2004 at his Office from one Sri B. Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, age: 40 years, occupation: Teacher, r/o. Sadasivpet, Medak District for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing of house ownership certificate.

Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet,

Medak District will hereinafter be referred to as Accused Officer (A.O.) and Sri B.

Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, age: 40 years, occupation: Teacher, r/o. Sadasivpet,

Medak District will hereinafter be referred to as the complainant.

### 2. The Service particulars of the Accused Officer are as follows:
