Arrested on 9-2-2004 at 4.10 P.M. and produced before the Hon'ble Special Judge for SPE & ACB cases, Hyderabad.

### 3. Facts and Evidence:

a.  $ \underline{\text{Allegation:}} $

That A.O. demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1,000/- on 9-2-2004 at his office, from the complainant for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate.

## b. Facts Leading to Registration of Case:

Brief facts of the case are that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 AM the complainant came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. The complainant stated that he has been working as a School Assistant in Govt. Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, is a retired Teacher. They own a house bearing No.3-4-30 at Yedla Bazar, in Ward No.11 of Sadashivpet Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office, Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate. As his father is old, he approached the AO on behalf of his father at Sadashivpet Municipal Council office for house owner-ship certificate. Then the A.O. demanded Rs.1,000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later again when he met A.O. for the certificate, he ascertained from him (complainant) whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally A.O. stated that unless the bribe is paid, the application would not be processed and he informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe. Hence he came to ACB office at Sangareddy on 6-2-2004, gave a report to the Inspector and requested to take necessary action against A.O.

The Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, Medak District, Nizamabad Range caused discreet enquiries into the allegations and also about the genuineness of the compliant and came to know that the A.O. is awfully corrupt and enjoying bad reputation. Basing on the above complaint, the Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range, registered a case in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, u/s 7 of PC Act, 1988 on 9-2-2004 at 9.00 AM after observing all formalities and arrangements were made for laying a trap.

### 4. Pre Trap Actions :

During the course of investigation the Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy, secured the presence of two Govt. servants Viz., 1. Sri.Pandla Narayana, s/o Sri P.Ramaiah, age: 52 yrs. Occ: Asst. Gr-I, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, and 2. Sri.Tammala Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Sri Shivaiah, age: 46 yrs, Asst. Gr-III, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, and requested them to act as mediators in this case. Sri.P.Ramachandra Rao, Sri.Ch.Ravikanth, Inspectors, ACB, Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad and Sri.V.Vijaya Bhaskar, Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, were present. The Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, called the complainant and introduced him to the mediators and vice versa. The mediators have gone through the copy of the complaint dt.6-2-2004 given by the Complainant, verified the contents with him, found it to be correct and initialled on the same.

The complainant produced Rs.1000/- (100 X 10 = 1000/-) which he intended to offer as bribe to AO as per his demand at his office. On the instructions of the Inspector, ACB, one of the mediators collected the amount from the complainant, counted the currency notes and noted down their numbers and denominations. On the instructions of the Inspector, ACB, HC 831 Sri N.Manikya Reddy applied a thin layer of phenolphthalein powder to the said currency notes. The Inspector then got prepared sodium carbonate solution prepared in a glass tumbler and asked the H.C. who applied phenolphthalein powder to the currency notes, to rinse his fingers in the colourless sodium carbonate solution. When he did so, the colourless solution turned into pink colour. Thus the reaction of phenolphthalein powder with sodium carbonate solution was demonstrated and the significance of the test was explained. The Inspector ACB, directed the H.C. to keep the tainted currency notes of Rs.1000/- in the left side emptied pocket of complainant's shirt. The Inspector, ACB, instructed the complainant not to touch the tainted amount till it is paid to the AO on his demand and also instructed the

complainant to pay the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- to A.O. only on his demand but not other-wise. He was also instructed to give signal by coming out of the building to the trap party by wiping his face with his hand kerchief, in case the A.O. demands and accepts the bribe amount from him. The complainant was further instructed not to shake hands with the A.O. before or after payment of bribe amount and not to touch the tainted amount till it was paid to the A.O. on his demand.

The samples of phenolphthalein and sodium carbonate powders which were used in the demonstration were preserved in two separate covers, duly sealed and attested by the mediators and the Inspector.

The Inspector, ACB instructed Sri.Manikyareddy, HC to deposit the phenolphthalein powder box in the store room and stay back in the office. The mediators, the complainant and the ACB officials washed their hands with soap and water before leaving for trap to ensure that their hands are free from phenolphthalein powder.

A mediators' report was drafted in the office room of Inspector, ACB Medak District at Sangareddy on 9-2-2004 from 9.30 A.M. to 10.30 AM by one of the mediators and it was attested by the other mediator and Inspectors of ACB Nizamabad Range.

### 5. Sequence of Trap and recording of proceedings etc.

After completing the mediators' report – 1 in the office room of Inspector, ACB Medak district at Sangareddy, the complainant, the mediators and the ACB officials left the ACB office at 11.00 AM by an Ambassador car bearing No. AP-25U459 and two motor cycles bearing Nos. 1) AP-9-Q-2487, 2) AP-23-E-8154 and reached Sadashivpet town at about 12.00 Noon. After parking the vehicles on the road side near Govt. Ravinder Model High School, Sadashivpet, the complainant was instructed to go to the A.O. with the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- and pay the amount to him, only on his demand, but not otherwise. The entire trap party members followed the complainant and took vantage positions near the Municipal Office building and waited for the prearranged signal from the complainant. The complainant was allowed to go to the Municipal office building to comply with the demand of bribe by the A.O. in his office. He was seen entering into the building by the trap party at about 12-10 P.M.

6

At about 12-20 P.M. the complainant came out of the Municipal Council office building and gave the pre arranged signal by wiping his face with hand kerchief indicating the demand and acceptance of the bribe amount of Rs.1000/- by the A.O. On receipt of the signal, the ACB officials and the mediators rushed into the Municipal office building and found one person sitting in a chair in the south east corner of the staff room of Municipal Office. The ACB officials disclosed their identity, to the said person, introduced the mediators to him and ascertained the name and address of that person as Mamidi Janardhan s/o Sri Narsimuloo, (AO) age:50 yrs, Occ: Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, R/o D.No.6-3-34/1, Raghavendra Nagar, Sadashivpet, Medak District. The complainant was asked to wait outside for some time. Then the Inspector, ACB, questioned the A.O. just then whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person. On that the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation. Then the Inspector, ACB secured water in two glasses and got sodium carbonate solutions prepared and requested the A.O. to rinse his both hand fingers separately in the said sodium carbonate solutions. When he did so, the colourless solutions in which he rinsed his right hand fingers turned into pink colour and the solutions in which he rinsed his left hand fingers remained colourless. When the ACB Inspector asked the A.O. to produce the tainted currency which he just then demanded and accepted from the complainant, the A.O. stated that the money was with one Ravi. When ACB officials searched the pants pocket of the A.O., a bunch of 100/- rupee currency notes was found in his wearing pants right side pocket. On verification of the currency notes by one of the mediators Sri.P.Narayana, they were found to be Rs.1000/- (One thousand) in 100/-. Rupee denominations. The numbers of the currency notes tallied with the numbers of the currency notes mentioned in the mediators report - I drafted at the O/O Inspector, ACB, Medak at Sangareddy on 9-2-04. The above amount of Rs.1000/- was seized by the Inspector ACB. Sodium carbonate solution was prepared in another glass tumbler.

The ACB Inspector secured a new lunāī through his subordinate and reduested the A.O. to hand over his wearing pant by cladding the lungi. Accordingly, the A.O. handed over his wearing pants. When the inner flap of the right side pocket of A.O's pant was subjected to phenolphthalein test, it yielded positive result. The resultant solutions of the above three tests were transferred into three separate bottles, duly sealed and attested by the mediators. The wearing pants, "Vimal" made thick brown in colour of the A.O. was also seized by the ACB Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

When the ACB Inspector questioned the A.O., about the demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house owner-ship certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted to have accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily. On demand by the Inspector, ACB, the A.O. produced the application given by the father of complainant and also other related documents. On perusal of the application, it was found that a note was written in Telugu for the approval and it was also found "please collect the fee and issue the certificate" in green ink with signature and date 9-2-2004. It was also noticed that the applicant already paid Rs.100/- vide RB No.26/22516, dt.7-2-2004 and it was approved. Further the application dated:1-1-2004 of Sri B.Shankarappa of Sadashivpet which was marked to "A1" Section vide inward registration No.324/2-1-04, was found enclosed to the note file along with copy of receipt of property tax paid by one Sri Bodapally Bakkappa vide. Receipt No.012842, copy of certificate Nos A1/MISC/96, dt.20-4-96 issued by the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet Municipality, duly certifying the house bearing No.3-4-30, stands in the name of Sri Bodapally Bakkappa and original certificate in Lr.No.A1/324/2004, dt.7-2-2004 issued by Commissioner Municipal Council, Sadashivpet on 9-2-2004 duly certified that the house bearing Municipal No.3-4-30 stands in the name of Sri B.Bakkappa addressed to Sri.B.Shankarappa, s/o Bakkappa, r/o Sadashivpet. The note file and enclosed documents containing sheet No 1 to 5 were seized by the ACB Inspector, duly attested by the mediators. The A.O. produced miscellaneous receipt book also. On perusal it was found the book is being maintained in book No.MF No.52, dt. Nil. The book containing receipts Nos.22501 to 22600 in duplicate upto Receipt No.2516 were written. The receipt No.22516 was written in the name of Sri.B.Shankarappa, s/o Sri Bakkappa, duly acknowledging receipt of Rs.100/- towards issue of owner ship certificate duly signed by concerned clerk on behalf of the Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet. The miscellaneous receipt book was seized by the Inspector, ACB duly attested by the mediators.

Then the inspector called the complainant and asked him to narrate as to what had all happened, after he left the trap party to go to the A.O. to meet his demand of bribe. The complainant stated that he went to the seat of the A.O. in the office and found him in his chair. He wished him and enquired about his work. Then the A.O. enquired whether he brought the demanded bribe amount of Rs.1,000/-, on which he

replied positively. Then the A.O. took out his application with related documents, after writing 3 or 4 lines in a paper, went to the chambers of the Municipal Commissioner and returned within two or three minutes. Later the A.O., took him nearer to bath room adjacent to the office, and demanded him to pay the bribe amount of Rs.1,000/-. Then he took out the tainted amount of Rs.1000/- from his shirt pocket and paid to the A.O. who received the amount with his right hand, and kept it in his wearing pant right side pocket. Then the A.O. and himself came back to his seat and the A.O. issued him the house ownership certificate dt.9-2-2004 duly signed by the Commissioner. So saying the complaint produced the certificate. On perusal of the certificate, it was noticed that the certificate was issued by the Commissioner on 9-2-2004, counter signed by the A.O. on 9-2-2004 in Lr.No.A1/324/2004, dt.7-2-2004 duly certifying that the house bearing Municipal No.3-4-30 stands in the name of Sri Bakkappa, Sadashivpet. As the office copy of the certificate was already seized along with note file, the certificate was returned to the complainant to avoid inconvenience.

The ACB Inspector examined the Municipal Commissioner who was found at his chamber at the time of trap. On examination Sri.R.Kistaiah, s/o Sri Balaiah, age:53 yrs, Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, stated that he has been working as Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet since 5-2-2004. The A.O. has been working as Jr.Asst. in his office. On 9-2-2004 at about 12.15 PM while he was in his office chamber, the A.O., came to him for approval of a note file pertaining to issue of house ownership certificate to one Sri B.Shankarappa. On the note file he wrote to collect the fee and issue certificate. Later within five minutes the A.O. came with the note file stating that the fee of Rs.100/- was already paid by the applicant on 7-2-2004. Immediately he approved the note file and signed the certificate in duplicate and put date 9-2-2004. The A.O. used to deal with subjects such as issue of ownership certificates, mutation certificates, assessment and collection of revenue. For issue of house owner-ship certificate, the applicant has to give an application in writing duly enclosing a copy of house tax paid receipt. After receiving the application the Commissioner marks the same to the concerned section after entering in inward register. The clerk concerned after receiving the application, puts up a note file after verification of office records for his approval and the certificate is issued if the applicant has no dues of taxes. As per the existing rules, house ownership certificate has to be issued within 5 days from the date of receipt of application from the applicant. In the instant case, the applicant Sri.B.Shankarappa submitted his application on 2-1-04 vide

9

inward register entry No.324, dated.02-01-2004. The application was said to be given to the A.O. for verification and put up on 4-1-2004. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with the A.O. without any valid reasons. The statement of the Commissioner was recorded by the Inspector, ACB.

On the request of the ACB Inspector, the Commissioner produced the Attendance Register of his staff. On perusal it was noticed that the register was maintained from Jan-2003 to 9-2-2004. On 9-2-2004 the A.O. signed in the Attendance Register. The Attendance Register was seized by the Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

Another Junior Asst. was also found in a chair in the south west corner of the staff room. On examination, the Junior Assistant Sri.B.Satyanarayana s/o Sri B.Laxmaiah age, 56 yrs, r/o Sadashivpet stated that he has been working as Junior Asst. since 14-11-2003. He used to deal with inward/outward sections and issue of Birth and Death certificates. On 2-1-2004 he received an application from one B.Ravi of Sadashivpet duly endorsed by the then Commissioner. He entered the particulars in inward register vide SI.No.324. As the clerk concerned, the A.O. was not available in his seat on 2-1-2004, he handed over the application to him on 4-1-2004 for further action at his end. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with the A.O. He further added that he did not witness the transaction that took place between the A.O. and the complainant. When the A.O. was shouting after the arrival of ACB officials he came to know about the trap. The statement of Sri.B.Satyanarayana, Jr. Asst., was recorded by the ACB officials. On the request of the ACB Inspector Sri.B.Satyanarayana Jr. Asst. produced the inward register from his custody. On perusal of the register, it was noticed that the register was maintained from 25-10-2002 to 3-2-2004. In page No.153 of the register, the application particulars in the instant case (B.Shankarappa, H.no.3-4-304, owner ship certificate A1) were entered vide. SI.No.324. The inward register containing page Nos.1 to 170 was seized by the ACB Inspector duly attested by the mediators.

The Inspector, ACB confronted the A.O. with the version of complainant and the records about the demand and acceptance of bribe from the complainant. The A.O. reiterated his earlier statement that he did not demand any bribe from the complainant.

but accepted an amount of Rs.1000/- from the complainant when he offered the amount voluntarily.

A rough sketch of the scene of offence i.e. Municipal office building Sadashivpet was drawn showing the seat of the A.O. and position of the trap party and mediators out side the building and it was attested by the mediators. The sample of sodium carbonate powder used for preparation of solutions was preserved in a cover, duly sealed and attested by the mediators.

The A.O. was arrested by the Inspector, ACB at 4.10 PM on 9.2.2004 after informing the grounds of his arrest.

A detailed proceedings (Mediators Report-II) was drafted in the staff room of O/o Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District by one of the mediators on 9-2-2004 from 12.40 PM to 4.15 PM by incorporating the above facts and it was attested by the other mediator, and Inspectors of ACB, Nizamabad Range. A copy of the mediators' report-II was supplied to the accused officer under proper acknowledgement.

### 6. Post Trap Actions:

The Inspectors ACB, Nizamabad Range searched the house of A.O. situated at D.No.6-3-34/1, Raghavendra Nagar, Sadashivpet, Medak District in the presence of Mediators on 9-2-2004 and found no incriminating material or worth while assets.

The A.O. was produced before the Hon'ble Judge for SPE & ACB cases,

Hyderabad on 10-2-2004 for Judicial custody and he was released on bail on

11-2-2004.

The statement of the complainant u/s 164 Cr.P.C. was recorded by the Hon'ble JFCM & Special Mobile Magistrate, Sangareddy on 21-2-2004.

### 7. Oral Evidence:

During the course of investigation the following witnesses were examined and their statements were recorded.

1. Statement of Sri.Bodapally Ravi, s/o Sri Shanakarappa, age: 40 yrs, occ: School Assistant r/o D.No.3-4-30, Sadashivpet, Medak district.

He is the complainant in this case. His statement u/s 161Cr.P.C. was recorded in detail. The complainant corroborated the contents of the FIR and narrated the pre-trap and post trap events in sequence. He also fared well in his 164 Cr.P.C. statement also recorded by the JFCM and Special Mobile Magistrate, Sangareddy.

### 2. Statement of Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, S/o Sri Bakkappa, age: 68 yrs, Occ: Retd Teacher R/o D.No.3-4-30, Sadashivpet, Medak district.

He is the father of the complainant. He stated that he had a house bearing No. 3-4-30 in Sadashivpet town. The house is in the name of his father Sri Bakkappa. He is the only legal heir to the property. On 2-1-2004 he along with his son Ravi (complainant) went to Municipal Office, Sadashivpet and gave an application to the Commissioner for issue of house owner ship certificate. The then Municipal Commissioner endorsed the application to A1 section and directed them to hand over the application in inward section. Accordingly on the same day they handed over the application to the Jr. Asst. at inward section. Later on, his son (complainant) approached the Municipal office for the certificate. He further stated that his son informed him that the concerned clerk in the office was demanding Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his application. But he told his son not to pay the bribe to the concerned clerk and report the matter to the ACB officials. On 9-2-2004 evening he came to know that the A.O. was trapped by ACB officials when he demanded and accepted a bribe of Rs.1000/- from his son and etc., facts

### 3. Statement of Sri.R.Kistaiah, s/o Sri Balaiah, age:53 yrs, Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak Dist.

He stated that he has been working as Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet since 5-2-2004. The A.O. has been working as Jr.Asst. in his office. On 9-2-2004 at about 12.15 PM while he was in his office chamber, the A.O. came to him for approval of a note file pertaining to issue of house ownership certificate to one Sri B.Shankarappa. On the note file he wrote to collect the fee and issue certificate. Later within five minutes, the A.O. came with the note file stating that the fee of Rs.100/- was already paid by the applicant on 7-2-2004. Immediately he approved the note file and signed the certificate in duplicate and put date 9-2-2004. The A.O. used to deal

with subjects such as issue of ownership certificate, mutation certificate, assessment and collection of revenue. For issue of house ownership certificate, the applicant has to give an application in writing duly enclosing a copy of house tax paid receipt. After receiving the application, the Commissioner marks the same to the concerned section after entering in inward register. The concerned clerk, after receiving the application puts up a note file after verification of office records for his approval and the certificate is issued if the applicant has no dues of taxes. As per the existing rules, house ownership certificate has to be issued within 5 days from the date of receipt of application from the applicant. In the instant case, the applicant Sri.B.Shankarappa submitted his application on 2-1-04 vide inward register entry No.324, dated.02-01-2004. The application was said to be given to the A.O., on 4-1-2004 for verification and put up. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004 the application was pending with the A.O. without any valid reasons and etc., facts.

### 4. Statement of Sri.B.Satyanarayana s/o Sri B.Laxmaiah age,56yrs, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District.

He stated that he has been working as Junior Asst., in Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, since 14-11-2003. He used to deal with inward/outward sections and issue of Birth and Death certificates. On 2-1-2004 he received an application from one B.Ravi of Sadashivpet duly endorsed by the then Commissioner. He entered the particulars in inward register vide SI.No.324. As the concerned clerk (A1) the A.O. was not available in his seat on 2-1-2004 he has handed over the application to him on 4-1-2004 for further action at his end. From 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004, the application was pending with the A.O. He further added that he did not witness the transaction that took place between the A.O. and the complainant. When the A.O. was shouting after the arrival of ACB officials, he came to know about the trap. On the request of the ACB Inspector, he handed over the inward register from his custody and etc, facts.

5. Statement of Smt.Sheelam Pankaja, w/o Sri D.V.S.Sastri, age:27yrs, occ: Valuation Officer, circle-II, Municipal corporation, Hyderabad city, R/o 1-2-186, Shanthinagar, Adilabad District.

She stated that she has been working as valuation officer in Municipal Corporation, Hyderabad City since 28-1-2004. Previously she worked as Municipal Commissioner, Sadashivpet Municipality, Medak district from 9-7-2001 to 27-1-2004. The A.O., has worked in the Sadashivpet Municipality during her tenure. He used to

look after the revenue section and process the applications pertaining to mutation, property ownership certificates, property assessments, etc., On 2-1-2004, she received an application from one Sri B.Shankarappa of Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate and endorsed the same to A1 section which was being looked after by the A.O. From 3-1-2004 to 23-1-2004 she was on leave. On 24-1-2004 she reported for duty and relieved from the Municipality on 27-1-2004. But the A.O. did not put up the note file for issue of house owner ship certificate to Sri B.Shankarappa and etc, facts.

6. Sri.D.Narsinga Rao, Judicial First Class Magistrate, Sangareddy, Medak District, Special Mobile Magistrate.

He recorded the statement of the complainant u/s 164 Cr.P.C. on 21-2-2004.

7. Sri. Pandla Narayana S/0 Sri P.Ramaiah, Age:62 Yrs, Assistant Gr.I, O/o District Manager, Civil Supplies Department, Sangareddy.

He is one of the mediators and will speak the facts of mediators report-I and mediators report-II in this case.

8. Sri.T.Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Sri Shiavaiah, age:46 yrs, Assistant Gr.III, O/o District Manager, Civil Supplies Department, Sangareddy.

He is another mediator and will speak the facts of mediators report-I and mediators report-II in this case.

### 8. Documentary Evidence:

1 Complaint dt.6-2-2004 of Sri. Bodapally Rav (Complainant) given to Inspector. ACB, Sangareddy.

2 FIR dated.9-2-2004

3 Mediators' report-1 dated. 9-2-2004

4 Mediators' report-2 dated. 9-2-2004

5 Rough sketch of the scene of offence dated. 9-2-2004

OC×C×G

6 The miscellaneous receipt book (book containing receipts Nos.22501 to 22600 in duplicate), containing the receipt written by A.O. in the name of the father of the complainant.

7 Note file pertaining to the father of the complainant containing 1 to 5 sheets was seized from the possession of the A.O on 9-2-2004. It reveals the pending official favour with the A.O.

8 The inward register containing page Nos.1 to 170, wherein the particulars of the application submitted by the complainant's father were entered in Page. No.153 vide SI.No.324, dt.2-1-2004.

9. The Attendance Register of the staff of Commissioner, Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, Medak District. It shows the presence of A.O. in the office on the

date of trap.

10. Copy of 164 Cr.P.C. Statement of complainant.

11. Letter in ROC No.C1/MCS/2004, Dated:9-3-2004 of the commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet Medak district. The Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet informed through this letter that the A.O. has not submitted his annual property statements to his higher authorities from date of his appointment to till 9-3-2004 and but acquired a house bearing No.6-3-34/4/1M, worth about Rs.6 lakhs in his name of A.O., one plot No.6-3-10 200 Sq yards worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of Smt.M. Suguna, wife of A.O. in Sadashivapet municipality, besides a scooter bearing No.AP23 7255.

### 9. Explanation of the Accused Officer:

immediately after the trap, when the Inspector questioned the A.O. whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person just then, the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand-fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation.

Again when the inspector questioned the A.O. about demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted that he accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, when offered by him voluntarily.

On 25-2-2004 the A.O submitted his written explanation wherein he contended that he was falsely implicated in this case. He further mentioned in his explanation that neither he demanded nor accepted any bribe from the complainant at any point of time. Finally he requested to drop all further proceedings against him in this case.

### 10. Rebuttal to the Explanation of the A.O.:

The A.O. has taken wavering pieas. immediately after the trap, when the Inspector questioned the A.O. whether he demanded and accepted any bribe from any person, the A.O. became nervous, tried to rub his hand-fingers to his pants and did not give any explanation.

Again when the Inspector questioned the A.O. about demand and acceptance of bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant for processing the application of the complainant's father for issuing house ownership certificate, the A.O. denied to have demanded any bribe from the complainant, but admitted that he accepted the amount of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant, which was offered by him voluntarily.

Fact is that on 4-1-2004 the A.O. received the application of the complainant for process to put up to the Commissioner. As per the standing instructions the work of the complainant was to be completed within 5 days from the date of receipt of the application. The Municipal Commissioner Sri.R.Kistalaiah, stated that the file of the complainant was pending with the A.O. from 4-1-2004 to 9-2-2004. The A.O. kept the file pending for more than a month with an intention to extract bribe from the complainant. On 9-2-2004 soon after receipt of the bribe amount, the A.O. delivered the house owner ship certificate to the complainant. Prior to the trap complainant met many times to AO but he demanded bribe from the complainant to issue house ownership certificate to complainant, hence contention of AO., that he accepted the amount given voluntarily by complainant can't be accepted.

The contention of the A.O. in his written explanation that neither he demanded nor accepted any bribe from the complainant at any point of time is nothing but false. The A.O. refused to process the application of father of complainant till the bribe was

paid to him. Whenever the complainant approached the A.O. for his work he told the complainant that the application will be processed as and when the bribe was paid and that unless the bribe is paid, the application would not be processed and on the day of trap after receiving the bribe amount from complainant A.O. issued house ownership certificate to complainant.

Facts of F.I.R., Mediators' Reports I & II, recovery of the tainted amount from the A.O.'s pant pocket, positive chemical tests on the right hand-fingers of the A.O. and the inner-flap of his pant pocket, and the recovery of pending file with the A.O. at the time of trap are all conclusive proof that the A.O. demanded and accepted Rs.1,000/- as bribe from the complainant.

### 11. FINDINGS & RECOMMENDATIONS:

In view of the oral and documentary evidence, it is established beyond reasonable doubt that the A.O. Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1,000/- from the complainant Sri B. Ravi, S/o. Sri Shankarappa, a Teacher of Sadasivpet, Nizamabad District on 9-2-2004 for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house ownership certificate.

It is, therefore, recommended that the A.O. may be prosecuted in a Court of Law u/Ss. 7 & 13(2), r/w. Sec. 13(1)(d) of the Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

Further, the A.O. acquired a house bearing No. 6-3-34/4/1M worth Rs.6 lakhs in his name and a plot bearing No.6-3-10 worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of his wife in Sadasivpet Municipality, besides a scooter bearing No. AP 23 7255 without obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed to submit his annual property statements from the date of his appointment to till 9-2-2004.

Hence, departmental action may be initiated against the A.O. for the lapses mentioned above.

#### ABSTRACT OF FINDINGS AND RECOMMENDATIONS:

# GOVERNMENT OF ANDHRA PRADESH ABSTRACT

Public Servant – Municipal Administration Department – Allegation of Corruption

against Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District – Trapped on 9-2-2004 – Sanction of Prosecution orders. - Issued.

MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION () DEPARTMENT

G.O.Rt/Ms.No:

Dated:

ORDER:

the category of

1. Whereas Sri.Mamidi Janrdhan, S/o Narsimulu, worked as Junior assistant to 22-2-04

Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from 5-12-2003 to till the date of suspension, as defined under

public servant within the meaning of clause (c) of Section 2 of Prevention of Corruption Act 1988. (Central Act 49 of 1988.)

2. And whereas it is alleged that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 AM the complainant Sri.B.Ravi, s/o Shankarappa, age:40yrs, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. The Gri B.Ravi complaint stated that he has been working as a school assistant in Govt. Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, a retired teacher. They own a house bearing No.3-4-30, at Yedla Bazar, in ward No.11 of Sadashivpet Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate. As his father is old, he approached the concerned clerk Sri.Janardhan, in Sadashivpet Municipal Council office on behalf of his father for house owner ship certificate. Sri.Janardhan demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later again when he met Sri.Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him (complainant) whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally Sri.Janardhan stated that unless the bribe is paid the application would not be processed and he informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe.

3. And whereas it is further alleged that Sri. B.Ravi approached Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District who demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe for processing the application of the complainant's father for issue of house owner ship certificate pertaining to his house

4. Whereas it is further alleged that Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District has demanded and accepted the bribe of Rs.1000/- from Sri.B.Ravi on 9-2-2004 at Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, as an illegal gratification other than legal remuneration and was trapped by the Inspector, Anti-corruption Bureau, Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004 after observing all the formalities required for a trap.

The Phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers of the Accused Officer, yielded positive result. The tainted currency notes were recovered from the pant right side pocket of Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District.

5. And whereas the said act of Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council,

Sadashivapet, Medak District constitutes an offence punishable under section 7

& 13(1),(d) r/w 13 (2) of Prevention of Corruption Act 1988.

6. And whereas the Government of Andhra Pradesh being the competent authority to remove the Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from service after fully and carefully examining the material placed before them in respect of the case consider the said aforesaid allegations and having regard to circumstances of the case consider that the said Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District be prosecuted in court of law for the aforesaid offences.

7. Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Clause (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 19 of P.C. Act 1988 (Central Act 49 of 1988.) the Government of Andhra Pradesh hereby accord sanction for prosecution of the said Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District for the aforesaid offences punishable under Section 7 & 13(1)(d) r/w 13(2) of P.C. Act 1988 and for any other cognate offences punishable under other provisions of law for the time being in force in respect of the above said acts and also for taking cognisance of the said offence by the court of competent jurisdiction.

##### ANNEXURE-1

STATEMENT OF ARTICLE OF CHARGES FRAMED AGAINST SRI.MAMIDI JANARDHAN.

FORMERLY JUNIOR ASSISTANT, MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SADASHIVAPET, MEDAK DISTRICT

That Sri.Mamdi Janardhan, S/o Narsimulu has joined Govt. Service on 10-09-1973. While functioning as Junior Asst., Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from 05-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 had committed grave misconduct in as much as he has acquired a house bearing No.6-3-34/4/1M, worth Rs.6 lakhs, in his name and a plot No.6-3-10 worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of his wife in Sadashivapet. Municipality besides a scooter bearing No.AP 23 7255 without obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed to submit his annual property statements from the date of his appointment to till 9-3-2004. Thus Sri.Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Assistant, has exhibited negligence of duties and thereby violated the provisions of A.P. Civil Service (Conduct) Rules, 1964.

Sri.Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Asst., Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District

by his above mentioned acts has exhibited lack of integrity, devotion to duty and conduct unbecoming

of a Member of Service and thereby contravened Rule 9 (1) (2) r/w 3(1) and (2) of A.P. Civil

Service (Conduct) Rules, 1964.

Appersoned,

m@Quay

141610y

AcC.AcD

Ada,

II

li

##### ANNEXURE-II

## STATEMENT OF IMPUTATION OF MISCONDUCT IN SUPPORT OF THE ARTICLE OF CHARGES FRAMED AGAINST SRI.MAMIDI JANARDHAN, FORMERLY JUNIOR ASSISTANT, MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SADASHIVAPET, MEDAK DISTRICT

That Sri Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Asst., Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District is native of Andole V&M, Medak District. His date of birth is 20-2-1951. He joined in Govt. Service on 10-9-1973 as Bill Collector. He has been working as Junior Asst., Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District since 5-12-2003 to till date of suspension 22-2-2004 vide Roc.No.34/2004-A2, Dt.17-2-2004 of the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad. His date of attaining superannuation is 28-2-2009. He is here in after referred to as Member of Service (MOS).

That Sri.Mamidi Janardhan, while working as Junior Asst., Municipal Council,

Sadashivpet, Medak District, from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 had committed grave misconduct in as

much as he has acquired a house bearing No.6-3-34/4/1M, worth Rs. 6 lakhs, in his name and a plot

No.6-3-10 worth about Rs.40,000/- in the name of his wife in sadashivpet, Municipality besides a

scooter bearing No.AP 23 7255 without obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed

to submit his annual property statements from the date of his appointment till 9-3-2004.

From the above facts and circumstances it is revealed the M.O.S. has failed to maintain absolute integrity while discharging the lawful duties allotted to him.

Thus Sri. Mamidi Janrdhan, by his above mentioned acts has exhibited lack of integrity devotion to duty conduct unbecoming of a Member of Service and thereby contravened Rule 9 (1) of 2) r/w 3(1) and (2) of A.P. Civil Service Conduct Rules, 1964.

Thus the Member of Service (M.O.S.) has therefore rendered himself liable for regular Departmental action for major Penalty.

# GOVERNMENT OF ANDHRA PRADESH ABSTRACT

Public Servants– Municipal Administration Department – Allegation of Corruption against Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District – Trapped on 9-2-2004 – Sanction of Prosecution orders. - Issued.

MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION () DEPARTMENT

G.O.RtMs. No:

ORDER:

Dated:

1. Whereas Sri.Mamidi Janradhan, S/o Narsimulu, worked as Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 i.e., the date of suspension. By virtue of the post held by him, he falls within the category of public servant as defined under clause (c) of Section 2 of Prevention of Corruption Act 1988. (Central Act 49 of 1988.)

2. And whereas it is alleged that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 AM Sri.B.Ravi, s/o Shankarappa, age:40 years, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. Sri B.Ravi stated that he has been working as a school assistant in Govt. Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa, a retired teacher owns a house bearing No.3-4-30, at Yedla Bazar, in ward No.11 of Sadashivpet Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office, Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate pertaining to the house. As his father happened to be an old man he approached the concerned clerk Sri.Janardhan, in Sadashivpet Municipal Council office on behalf of his father for owner ship certificate. Sri.Janardhan demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later again when he met Sri.Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally Sri.Janardhan stated that unless the bribe was paid the application would

not be processed and he informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe.

3. And whereas it is further alleged that Sri. B.Ravi approached Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District who demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe for processing the application of his father for issue of owner ship certificate pertaining to his house.

4. Whereas it is further alleged that Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District has demanded and accepted the bribe of Rs.1000/- from Sri.B.Ravi on 9-2-2004 at Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, as an illegal gratification other than legal remuneration and was trapped by the Inspector, Anti-Corruption Bureau, Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004 after observing all the formalities required for a trap. The Phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers of the Accused Officer, yielded positive result. The tainted currency notes were recovered at his instance from the right side pant pocket of Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District.

5. And whereas the said act of Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst, Municipal Council,

Sadashivapet, Medak District constitutes an offence punishable under section 7

&13(1),(d) r/w 13 (2) of Prevention of Corruption Act 1988.

6. And whereas the Government of Andhra Pradesh being the competent authority to remove the Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst., Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from service after fully and carefully examining the material placed before them in respect of the case consider the said aforesaid allegations and having regard to circumstances of the case consider that the said Sri.M.Janardhan, Jr.Asst., Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District be prosecuted in court of law for the aforesaid offences.

7. Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Clause (b) of Sub-section (1) of Section 19 of P.C. Act 1988 (Central Act 49 of 1988.) the Government of Andhra Pradesh hereby accord sanction for prosecution of the said Sri.M.Janardhan. Jr.Asst, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District for the aforesaid offences punishable under Sections 7 & 13(1)(d), r/w 13(2) of P.C. Act 1988 and for any other cognate offences punishable under other provisions of law for the time being in force in respect of the above said acts and also for taking cognizance of the said offence by the court of competent jurisdiction.

#### (BY ORDER IN THE NAME OF THE GOVERNOR OF ANDHRA PRADESH)

PRINCIPAL SECRETARY TO GOVERNMENT MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATIVE DEPARTMENT.

To

The Director General,

Anti-Corruption Bureau,

Andhra Pradesh,

Hyderabad

Thus Sri.Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Assistant, has exhibited negligence of duties and thereby violated the provisions of A.P. Civil Service (Conduct) Rules, 1964.

##### ORDER :-

1. Whereas Sri Mamidi Janardhan, S/o Narsimulu, worked as Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 i.e., the date of suspension. By virtue of the post held by him, he falls within the category of public servant as defined under clause (c) of Section 2 of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 (Central Act 49 of 1988).

2. And whereas it is alleged that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 A.M. Sri B.Ravi, S/o Shankarappa, age: 40 years, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadasivapet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, A.C.B., Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. Sri B.Ravi stated that he has been working as a school assistant in Government Ravindra Model School, Sadasivapet. His father Sri Bodapally Shankarappa, a retired teacher owns a house bearing No.3-4-30, at Yedla Bazar, in Ward No.11 of Sadasivapet Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal Office, Sadasivapet for issue of house ownership certificate pertaining to his house. As his father happened to be an old man he approached the concerned clerk Sri Janardhan, in Sadasivapet Municipal Council office on behalf of his father for ownership certificate. Sri Janardhan demanded Rs.1,000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later, again when he met Sri Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally, Sri Janardhan stated that unless the bribe was paid the application would not be processed and he informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe.

3. And whereas it is further alleged that Sri B. Ravi approached Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District who demanded Rs.1,000/- as bribe for processing the application of his father for issue of ownership certificate pertaining to his house.

4. Whereas it is further alleged that Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District has demanded and accepted the bribe of Rs.1,000/ from Sri B. Ravi on 9-2-2004 at Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, as an illegal gratification other than legal remuneration and was trapped by the Inspector, Anti-Corruption Bureau, Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range in Cr. No. 5/ACB-NZB/2004 after

observing all the formalities required for a trap. The Phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers of the Accused Officer, yielded positive result. The tainted currency notes were recovered at his instance from the right side pant pocket of Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District.

5. And whereas the said act of Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District constitutes an offence punishable under section 7 and 13(1), (d) read with 13(2) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

6. And whereas, I, K. Vema Reddy, Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad being the competent authority to remove the said Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District from service after fully and carefully examining the material placed before me, in respect of the case consider the said aforesaid allegations and having regard to the circumstances of the case consider that the said Sri M. Sub:- Public Services - Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s),

Sadasivpet Municipality - Acquired properties without obtaining permission from the competent authority - Proceedings under Rule 20 of APCS (CCA) Rules 1991 - Articles of charges - issued.

Ref: - Lr.No.50/RCT-NMD/2004, dated 12-7-2004 of the Director General, A.C.B, A.P.Hyderabad.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//f8da22c1-426f-425e-9ebb-88cd484ee627/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_2811_1651_3161_2287.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A11Z%2F-1%2F%2F377284d780dcef7d5b7328ccc4674fb7097c9e7fb9827fbbb8b4efb3ecc1cd1e" alt="Image" width="10%" /></div>


It is proposed to hold an enquiry against Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Sadasivpet Municipality in accordance with procedure laid down in rule 20 of A.P.Civil Services (CCA). Rules 1991.

The substance of the imputations of misconduct or misbehavior in respect of which the inquiry is proposed to be held is set out in the enclosed statement of articles of charges and imputations (Annexure-I and II). A list of documents by which, and a list of witness by whom, the article of charges are proposed to be sustained are also enclosed (Annexure-III & IV).

Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Sadasivpet Municipality is directed to  $ \underset{\cdot}{s} $ubmit within 10 days of the receipt of this order, a written statement of his defence.

Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), is informed that an enquiry will be held in respect of those charges as are not admitted. He should, therefore, specially admit or deny each article of charge.

SAD Charge memo doc

Sri.M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s) is further informed that if he does not submit his written statement of defence on or before the date specified in para-3 above further action will be processed based on the material available.

Attention of Sri.M.Janardhan is invited to rule 24 of the A.P.C.S (Conduct) Rules, 1964, under which no Government Servant shall bring or attempt to bring any political or outside influence to bear upon any superior authority to further his interests in respect of matter pertaining to his service under the Government. If any representation is received on his behalf from another person in respect of any matter dealt within those proceedings it will be presumed that Sri.M.Janardhan aware of such a representation and that it has been made at his instance and action will be taken against him for violation of Rule 24 of A.P.C.S. (Conduct) Rules, 1964.

The receipt of the memorandum may be acknowledged.

## Sd/- K.VENKATARAMI REDDY REGIONAL DIRECTOR-CUM-APPELLATE COMMISSIONER OF MUNICIPAL ADMN., HYDERABAD.

##### ANNEXURE-I

## STATEMENT OF ARTICLE OF CHARGES FRAMED AGAINST SRI MAMDI JANARDHAN, FORMERLY JUNIOR ASSISTANT, MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SADASIVAPET, MEDAK DISTRICT.

CHARGE NO. 1.

That, Sri. Mamdi Janardhan, S/o Narsimulu has joined Government Service on 10-9-1973. While functioning as Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District from 05-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 had committed grave misconduct in as much as he has acquired a house bearing No. 6-3-34/4/1M, worth of Rs. 6.00 Lakhs, in his name and a plot No. 6-3-10 worth about Rs. 40,000/- in the name of his wife in Sadasivapet Municipality besides a scooter bearing No. AP 23 7255 without obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed to submit his annual property statements from the date of his appointment till 9-3-2004. Thus Sri. Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Assistant, has exhibited negligence of duties and thereby violated the provisions of A.P.Civil Service (Conduct) Rules, 1964.

 $ \underline{\text{CHARGE NO. 2.}} $

That, Sri. Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District by his above mentioned acts has exhibited lack of integrity, devotion to duty and conduct unbecoming of a Member of Service and thereby contravened Rule 9 (1) and 2 (2) read with 3(1) and (2) of A.P. Civil Service (Conduct Rules, 1964.

Sd/- K.VENKATARAMI REDDY

REGIONAL DIRECTOR-CUM-APPELLATE

COMMISSIONER OF MUNICIPAL ADMN.,

HYDERABAD

##### ANNEXURE-II

## STATEMENT OF IMPUTATION OF MISCONDUCT IN SUPPORT OF ARTICLE OF CHARGES FRAMED AGAINST SRI. MAMDI JANARDHAN, FORMERLY JUNIOR ASSISTANT, MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SADASIVAPET, MEDAK DISTRICT.

That, Sri.Mamdi Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District is native of Andole V&M, Medak District. His date of birth is 20-2-1951. He joined in Government Service on 10-9-1973 as Bill Collector. He has been working as Junior Asst., Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District since 5-12-2003 till date of suspension 22-2-2004 vide Roc.No. 34/2004-A2, Dt. 17-2-2004 of the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad. His date of attaining superannuation is 28-2-2009. He is here in after referred to as Member of Service (MOS).

That, Sri.Mamdi Janardhan, while working as Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District, from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 had committed grave misconduct in as much as he has acquired a house bearing No. 6-3-34/4/1M, worth Rs. 6.00 lakhs, in his name and a plot No. 6-3-10 worth Rs. 40,000/- in the name of his wife in Sadasivapet Municipality, besides a scooter bearing No. AP 23 7255 without obtaining permission from the competent authority and failed to submit his annual property statements from the date of his appointment till 9-3-2004.

From the above facts and circumstances, it is revealed the M.O.S. has failed to maintain absolute integrity while discharging the lawful duties allotted to him.

Thus, Sri. Mamdi Janardhan, by his above mentioned acts has exhibited lack of integrity devotion to duty conduct unbecoming of a Member of Service and thereby contravened Rule 9 (1) and 2(2) read with 3(1) and (2) of A.P. Civil Service Conduct Rules 1964.

Thus, the Member of Service (M.O.S.) has therefore rendered himself liable for regular Departmental action for major Penalty.

Sd/-K.VENKATARAMI REDDY

REGIONAL DIRECTOR-CUM-APPELLATE

COMMISSIONER OF MUNICIPAL ADMN.,

HYDERABAD

# PROCEEDINGS OF THE REGIONAL DIRECTOR-CUM-APPELLATE COMMISSIONER OF MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION, HYDERABAD.

PRESENT : SRI K. VENKATA RAMI REDDY.

Roc.No. 34/2004/A2

Dated: 24-7-2004.

Public Servants – Municipal Administration Department – Allegation of Corruption against Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District – Trapped on 9-2-2004 – Sanction of Prosecution orders – Issued.

##### ORDER :-

1. Whereas Sri Mamidi Janardhan, S/o Narsimulu, worked as Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 i.e., the date of suspension. By virtue of the post held by him, he falls within the category of public servant as defined under clause (c) of Section 2 of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 (Central Act 49 of 1988).

2. And whereas it is alleged that on 06-02-2004 at 10-00 A.M. Sri B.Ravi, S/o Shankarappa, age: 40 years, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadasivapet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, A.C.B., Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu. Sri B.Ravi stated that he has been working as a school assistant in Government Ravindra Model School, Sadasivapet. His father Sri Bodapally Shankarappa, a retired teacher owns a house bearing No.3-4-30, at Yedla Bazar, in Ward No.11 of Sadasivapet Municipality. His father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal Office, Sadasivapet for issue of house ownership certificate pertaining to his house. As his father happened to be an old man he approached the concerned clerk Sri Janardhan, in Sadasivapet Municipal Council office on behalf of his father for ownership certificate. Sri Janardhan demanded Rs.1,000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later, again when he met Sri Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally, Sri Janardhan stated that unless the bribe was paid the application would not be processed and he informed the same to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe.

3. And whereas it is further alleged that Sri B. Ravi approached Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District who demanded Rs.1,000/- as bribe for processing the application of his father for issue of ownership certificate pertaining to his house.

4. Whereas it is further alleged that Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District has demanded and accepted the bribe of Rs.1,000/- from Sri B. Ravi on 9-2-2004 at Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, as an illegal gratification other than legal remuneration and was trapped by the Inspector, Anti-Corruption Bureau, Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range in Cr. No. 5/ACB-NZB/2004 after

janardhan-prosecution orders.doc

observing all the formalities required for a trap. The Phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers of the Accused Officer, yielded positive result. The tainted currency notes were recovered at his instance from the right side pant pocket of Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District.

5. And whereas the said act of Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District constitutes an offence punishable under section 7 and 13(1), (d) read with 13(2) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

6. And whereas, I, K. Venkata Rami Reddy, Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad being the competent authority to remove the said Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District from service after fully and carefully examining the material placed before me, in respect of the case consider the said aforesaid allegations and having regard to the circumstances of the case consider that the said Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District be prosecuted in court of law for the aforesaid offences.

7. Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Clause (c) of Sub-section (1) of Section 19 of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 (Central Act 49 of 1988), I do hereby accord sanction for prosecution of the said Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District for the aforesaid offences punishable under Sections 7 and 13 (1)(d), read with 13 (2) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 and for any other cognizable offences punishable under other provisions of law for the time being in force in respect of the above said acts and also for taking cognizance of the said offences by the court of competent jurisdiction.

COMMISSIONER OF MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION, HYDERABAD.

To

The Director General,

Anti Corruption Bureau,

Andhra Pradesh, Hyderabad.

Copy submitted to the Commissioner and Director of Municipal Administration, A.P., Hyderabad for favour of information. (2772/2004/E4)

Copy submitted to the Principal Secretary to Government, M.A. and U.D. Department, A.P. Secretariat, Hyderabad for favour of information.

# IN THE COURT OF THE HONOURABLE PRINCIPAL SPECIAL JUDGE FOR SPE & ACB CASES AT HYDERABAD. C.C.No. 12004 IN RC NO 5/ACB-NZR/2004

# RC. NO. 5/ACB-NZB/2004

##### Between:

State through Inspector of Police,

Anti-Corruption Bureau,

Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad.

Complainant.

##### And

Sri. Mamidi Janardhan,

s/o Narsimulu,

Junior Assistant, O/o Municipal Commissioner,

Municipal Council,

Sadashivapet, Medak District,

R/o H. No.6-3-34/4/1, Raghavendra Colony,

Sadashivapet, Medak District

Accused Officer

Charge sheet filed in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, u/s 7, 13(2), r/w 13(1) (d), of the P.C. Act 198

Honoured Sir,

That the A.O. Sri Mamidi Janrdhan, worked as Junior Assistant, office of the Municipal Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 and as such he is a public servant with in the meaning of Clause (C) of Sec.2 of PC Act 1988 (Central act 49 of 1988).

On 06-02-2004 at 10-00 AM the complainant (LW-1) Sri.B.Ravi, s/o Shankarappa, age:40 yrs, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu, alleging that he is working as a school assistant in Government Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa is a retired teacher. They own a house bearing No.3-4-30 at Yedla Bazar in ward No.11 of Sadashivpet Municipality and that his father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office, Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate pertaining to his house. Due to the old-age of his father, the complainant (LW-1) approached the concerned clerk Sri.Janardhan in Sadashivpet Municipal Office for the issuance of house owner ship certificate. Sri.Janardhan demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later, again when LW-1 met Sri.Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally Sri.Janardhan stated that unless the bribe was paid the application would not be processed. He informed the

same facts to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe. Hence he came to ACB office Sangareddy on 6-2-2004 and gave a report to the Inspector (LW-10) and requested to take necessary action against Sri. Janardhan, Clerk, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District.

On receiving the report of LW-1/and after due verification LW-10 registered the report of LW-1 as a case in Cr. No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, U/S 7 of PC Act, 1988, on 9-2-2004 at 9.00 AM against Sri. M. Janardhan, and took up investigation.

During the course of investigation LW-10 secured the presence of two Government Servants viz. Sri.Padma Narayana, s/o P.Ramaiah, age: 52 yrs. Occ: Asst. Gr-I, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, (LW-3) and . Sri.Tammala Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Shivaiah, age: 46 yrs, Asst. Gr-III, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, Medak district (LW-4) as mediators and after observing the necessary formalities, LW-10 with the assistance of LWs-11 & 12, and others laid the trap on 9-02-2004 at 12.40 P.M at Municipal office staff room, Sadashivpet, Medak District when the Junior Assistant (AO), demanded and accepted the bribe amount of Rs.1000/- from LW-1.

The phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers of the Accused Officer, yielded positive result and the tainted amount was recovered at his instance from the right side pant pocket of the accused officer. The accused officer was arrested and produced before Hon'ble Special Judge for SPE and ACB cases, Hyderabad for remand. The Accused Officer was remanded by the Hon'ble Court and later released on bail.

During course of investigation LW-10 examined and recorded the statements of Sri.B.Ravi (LW-1), Sri.R.Kistaiah (LW-5) and Sri.B.Satyanrayana (LW-6), LW-12 examined and recorded the statements of Sri.B.Shankarappa (LW-2) and Smt.Sheelam Parikaja (LW-7).

The Investigation Officer (LW-10) seized documents 1) Original Application of LW-1 along with note file, containing 5 sheets, 2) Miscellaneous Receipt Book, 3) Inward Register and 4) The Attendance Register of staff of Municipal Council, Sadashivapet.

During the course of investigation LW-10 filed a requisition before the Hon'ble Judicial First class Magistrate, Sangareddy Medak District and the statement of LW-1 U/s 164 Cr.P.C was recorded by LW 8 Sri.D.Narsinga Rao, Judicial First Class Magistrate, Sangareddy, Medak district.

Sri. K. Venkata Ram Reddy, Regional Director cum Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration,

Hyderabad being the competent authority to remove the AO from service had accorded sanction vide Proc.

ROC. No. 34/2004/A2 dt. 24-7-2004 to prosecute Sri. M. Janardhan in a Court of law for the aforesaid offence

and for taking cognizance of the offence.

Thus the facts and material gathered during the course of investigation revealed that the AO Sri.M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- on 9-2-2004 from LW1 as a motive for showing official favour of processing the application of LW-2 for issuing house owner ship certificate. Thus the AO has committed the offence punishable U/s 7, 13(2) r/w 13 (1) (d) of PC Act 1988.

Hence charge.

1. From the Director General, ACB Lr. No:50/RCT-NMD/2004, dt. 18.5.2004.

The final report of Bureau referred to 1 $ ^{st} $ above is sent herewith to the M.A. & U.D.Department.

2. Commission notes that the Regional Director-cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad accorded sanction for prosecution vide ref.3 $ ^{rd} $ cited. against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, J.A. O/o Municipal Commissioner Sadasivpet, Medak District (under suspension) in the trap case against him. Commission, therefore advises the M.A. & U.D. Department to pursue with the ACB to file charge sheet against the A.O. in the ACB Court immediately, if not already done.

3. Commission also requests the Department to intimate the stage of departmental action against to Sri Mamidi Janardhan, J.A. with regard to violation of conduct rules and failure to submit Annual Property Statements to 'the Department

4. Receipt of the letter together with final report of ACB may please be acknowledged and action taken in the matter may be intimated to the Commission.

Yours faithfully.

# IN THE COURT OF THE HONOURABLE PRINCIPAL SPECIAL JUDGE FOR SPE & ACB CASES AT HYDERABAD. C.C.No. /2004 IN RC. NO. 5/ACB-NZB/2004

Between:

State through Inspector of Police,

Anti-Corruption Bureau,

Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad.

Complainant.

##### And

Sri. Mamidi Janardhan,

s/o Narsimulu,

Junior Assistant, O/o Municipal Commissioner,

Municipal Council,

Sadashivapet, Medak District,

R/o H. No. 6-3-34/4/1, Raghavendra Colony,

Sadashivapet, Medak District

Accused Officer

Charge sheet filed in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, u/s 7, 13(2), r/w 13(1) (d), of the P.C. Act 1988.

Honoured Sir,

That the A.O. Sri.Mamidi Janrdhan, worked as Junior Assistant, office of the Municipal Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District from 5-12-2003 to 22-2-2004 and as such he is a public servant within the meaning of Clause (C) of Sec.2 of PC Act 1988 (Central Act 49 of 1988).

On 06-02-2004 at 10:00 AM the complainant (LW-1) Sri.B.Ravi, s/o Shankarappa, age:40 yrs, Occ: Teacher, r/o Sadashivpet, Medak District came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak District at Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range and gave a written complaint in Telugu, alleging that he is working as a school assistant in Government Ravindra Model School, Sadashivpet. His father Sri.Bodapally Shankarappa is a retired teacher. They own a house bearing No.3-4-30 at Yedla Bazar in ward No.11 of Sadashivpet Municipality and that his father gave an application on 2-1-2004 in the Municipal office, Sadashivpet for issue of house ownership certificate pertaining to his house. Due to the old-age of his father, the complainant (LW-1) approached the concerned clerk Sri.Janardhan in Sadashivpet Municipal Office for the issuance of house ownership certificate. Sri.Janardhan demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application. Later, again when LW-1 met Sri.Janardhan for the certificate, he ascertained from him

whether he brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid. Finally Sri Janardhan stated that unless the bribe was paid the application would not be processed. He informed the same facts to his father. His father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe. Hence he came to ACB office Sangareddy on 6-2-2004 and gave a report to the Inspector (LW-10) and requested to take necessary action against Sri Janardhan, Clerk, Municipal Council, Sadashivpet, Medak District.

On receiving the report of LW-1 and after due verification LW-10 registered the report of LW-1 as a case in Cr.No.5/ACB-NZB/2004, U/S 7 of PC Act, 1988, on 9-2-2004 at 9.00 AM against Sri.M. Janardhan, and took up investigation.

During the course of investigation LW-10 secured the presence of two Government Servants viz. Sri.Padma Narayana, s/o P.Ramaiah, age: 52 yrs. Occ: Asst. Gr-I, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, (LW-3) and. Sri.Tammala Shivaiah Balraj, s/o Shivaiah, age: 46 yrs, Asst. Gr-III, O/o DM, Civil Supplies Dept., Sangareddy, Medak district (LW-4) as mediators and after observing the necessary formalities LW-10 with the assistance of LWs-11 & 12, and others laid the trap on 9-02-2004 at 12.40 P.M at Municipal office staff room, Sadashivpet, Medak District when the Junior Assistant (AO), demanded and accepted the bribe amount of Rs.1000/- from LW-1.

The phenolphthalein test conducted on the right hand fingers of the Accused Officer, yielded positive result and the tainted amount was recovered at his instance from the right side pant pocket of the accused officer. The accused officer was arrested and produced before Hon'ble Special Judge for SPE and ACB cases, Hyderabad for remand. The Accused Officer was remanded by the Hon'ble Court and later released on bail.

During course of investigation LW-10 examined and recorded the statements of Sri.B.Ravi (LW-1), Sri.R.Kistaiah (LW-5) and Sri.B.Satyanrayana (LW-6), LW-12 examined and recorded the statements of Sri.B.Shankarappa (LW-2) and Smt.Sheelam Pankaja (LW-7).

The Investigation Officer (LW-10) seized documents 1) Original Application of LW-1 along with note file, containing 5 sheets, 2) Miscellaneous Receipt Book, 3) Inward Register and 4) the Attendance Register of staff of Municipal Council, Sadashivapet.

During the course of investigation LW-10 filed a requisition before the Hon'ble Judicial First

class Magistrate, Sangareddy Medak District and the statement of LW-1 U/s 164 Cr.P.C was

recorded by LW 8 Sri.D.Narsinga Rao, Judicial First Class Magistrate, Sangareddy, Medak district.

Sri.K.Venkata Ram Reddy, Regional Director cum Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad being the competent authority to remove the AO from service had accorded sanction vide Proc. ROC.No.34/2004/A2 dt.24-7-2004 to prosecute Sri.M.Janardhan in a Court of law for the aforesaid offence and for taking cognizance of the offence.

Thus the facts and material gathered during the course of investigation revealed that the AO Sri.M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Municipal Council, Sadashivapet, Medak District demanded and accepted bribe of Rs.1000/- on 9-2-2004 from LW1 as a motive for showing official favour of processing the application of LW-2 for issuing house ownership certificate. Thus the AO has committed the offence punishable U/s 7, 13(2) r/w 13 (1) (d) of PC Act 1988.

Hence charge.

(P. Ramachandra Rao)

Inspector of Police,

Anti-Corruption Bureau,

Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad

Grade-11, O/o D.M. Civil Supplies Department, Sangareddy.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>To speak about the application of Sri.Shankarappa which was pending with the AO for about 37 days and the work of LW-2 should have been completed within 5 days and etc facts.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.R.Kistaiah, s/o Balaiah, Age:53 Yrs, Occ: Commissioner, Municipal council, Sadashivapet, Medak District.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>To speak about the application of LW-2 and handing over the same to Sri.M. Janardhan on 4-1-2004 and etc facts.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>6.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.B.Satyanarayana, s/o B.Laxmaiah, age:56yrs, Occ: Junior Assistant, O/o Municipal Council, R/o Sadashivpet, Medak District.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>To speak about the application received from one B.Shankarappa for issue of house ownership certificate and endorsed the same to AO</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>7.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Smt.Sheelam Pankaja, w/o D.V.S.Sastri, age:27yrs, Occ:Valuation Officer Circle-11, Municipal Corporation, Hyderabad City, r/o 1-2-186, Shanthinagr, Adilabad District</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.M. Janardhan and etc facts.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>8.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.D.Narsinga Rao, Judicial First Class Magistrate, Sangareddy Medak District</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Recorded the statement of complainant u/s 164 Cr.P.C.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>9.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.K.Venkata Ram Reddy, Regional Director Cum Appellate, Municipal Administration, Hyderabad</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Issued prosecution sanction order</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>10.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.V.Vijaya Bhasker, Inspector, ACB, Sangareddy, Nizamabad Range.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trap laying Officer and 1.0.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>11.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.Ch.Ravi Kanth, Inspector, ACB, Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Assisted the Trap laying Officer</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>12.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Sri.P.Ramachandra Rao, Inspector, ACB, Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>To speak about the investigation and filing charge sheet.</td></tr></table>

## C.No. 50/RCT-NMD/2004-S4. date 20-1-2005

##### Sir.

Sub: Public Servants --- Allegation of corruption --- Demand and acceptance of bribe by Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak District --- Trapped on 9-2-2004 --- Chargesheet filed in the Court --- Information --- Reg.

Ref: - 1) Final Report of the Bureau of even number, dated 18-5-2004.

2) Govt Memo No. 3890/Via IV 2/2004. dated 26.6.2004.

2) Govt. Memo. No. 3890/Vig. IV.2/2004-2, dated 26-6-2004.

3) This Office letter of even number, dated 12-7-2004.

4) Proceedings Roc. No. 34/2004/A2, dated 24-7-2004 & Letter Roc. No. 34/2004/A2, dated 24-7-2004 of the Regional Director-cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad.

5) Lr. No. 84/VC.D1/04-3, dated 21-9-2004 of the Vigilance Commissioner, A.P. Vigilance Commission, Hyderabad.

Pleas refer to the correspondence cited.

The Bureau filed a chargesheet against the Accused Officer Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Municipal Council, Sadasivapet, Medak District on 30-9-2004 in the Court of the Principal Special Judge for S.P.E. & A.C.B. Cases, Hyderabad and the case has been taken on file vide C.C.No. 42/2004.

Yours faithfully,

R.m. Home New

for Director General.

A.P., Hyderabad.

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is informed that a meeting to review the suspension cases pending in Municipal Administration Department for more than six months, one year, one and half year and more than two years is fixed on 18-5-2005 at 11.30 A.M. in the Chambers of Secretary to Government, Municipal Administration & Urban Development Department (Room No.104, L.Block, A.P.Secretariat). Krishna Murthy,</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>24-05-04</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The Hon&#x27;ble High Court in its order dt.9-3-2005 has directed to review the orders of suspension within a period of two months from the date of receipt of the copy of the order. However, if review are not undertaken and final orders are not passed by the Court within the time stipulated above, the orders of suspension shall not be given effect to and the applicant shall be continued in service.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1. REFER CRIME NO. 56/ACB-NZB/2004 AGAINST MAMIDI JANARIDHAN JUNIOR ASSISTANT OF MUNICIPAL COUNCIL, SADASHIVPET, MEDAK DISTRICT [NOW UNDER SUSPENSION] () SIGNAL INFORMATION ON FOLLOWING POINTS URGENTLY ()

1) WHETHER THE DELAY IN FINALISATION OF ENQUIRY PROCEEDINGS CANNOT BE ATTRIBUTED TO THE EMPLOYEE:

II) WHETHER THE SUSPENDED EMPLOYEE IS COOPERATING WITH THE PROSECUTION AGENCY IN FACILITATING THE ENQUIRY:

III) WHETHER THE SUSPENDED EMPLOYEE IS ATTENDING THE COURT WHENEVER SUMMONED FOR HEARING

IV) WHETHER PROSECUTION WITNESSES HAVE BEEN EXAMINED.

V) WHETHER REINSTATEMENT OF THE A.O. WILL HAVE EFFECT ON THE CASE

INFORM PRESENT STAGE OF CASE AND NEXT DATE OF POSTING ALSO (.)

SUB:- PUBLIC SERVICES - Municipal Administration Department - Review of suspension cases for more than Eighteen (18) months on 13-9-2005 at 11.30 A.M. - Intimation - Regarding.

##### -000

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is informed that a meeting to review the suspension cases pending in Municipal Administration Department for more than eighteen (18) months is fixed on 13-9-2005 at 11.30 A.M. in the Chambers of Secretary to Government, Municipal Administration & Urban Development Department (Room No.110, L.Block, A.P.Secretariat). The statement showing the suspension cases pending in the Department for more than eighteen (18) months is enclosed herewith. The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is therefore requested to depute the concerned officials to attend the review meeting with relevant material.

#### CHANDRA B. MALASI ADDITIONAL SECRETARY TO GOVERNMENT

To

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau, Hyderabad. (w.e.)

Copy to:

The P.S. to Secretary. (SPS)

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ba75924f-67ac-42c5-a04d-1a50a9239e7d/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_seal_box_895_15_2254_971.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A15Z%2F-1%2F%2F6343f8de4a72cab566ac84516eaa777c74a7149baba19d061d69c570689dd616" alt="Image" width="40%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ba75924f-67ac-42c5-a04d-1a50a9239e7d/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_seal_box_895_15_2254_971.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A15Z%2F-1%2F%2F6343f8de4a72cab566ac84516eaa777c74a7149baba19d061d69c570689dd616" alt="Image" width="40%" />  A.P. POLICE COMMUNIC  </div>   </div>


(3) WHETHER THE SUSPENDED EMPLOYEE IS AT TENDING THE COURT WHENVER SUBMONTED FOR HEARING: YES

(4) WHETHER PROSECUTION WITNESSES HAVE BEEN EXAMINED: NO WHETHER REINSTANT OF THE A.O. WILL HAVE EFFECT ON THE CASE: YES
