Hyderabad.

I invite your attention to the references fourth cited, wherein you have been requested the Commissioner & Director of Municipal Administration to file Caveat Petition through the concerned Government Pleader in the A.P. Administrative Tribunal and also in Hon'ble high Court of Andhra Pradesh with a view to retraining the Accused Officer from obtaining release orders from the A.P. Administrative Tribunal or the A.P. High Court.

Accordingly, in the reference fifth and sixth cited the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad has been requested to file caveat on behalf of Commissioner & Director of Municipal Administration in the A.P. Administrative Tribunal and also in Hon'ble high Court of Andhra Pradesh.

In reply to this office letters, the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad in the reference seventh cited have furnished a copy of Caveat Petition filed by him in A.P.A.T., Hyderabad.

# IN THE HON'BLE ANDHRA PRADESH ADMISTRATIVE TRIBUNAL : HYDERABAD.

# CAVEATE NO. (、OF 2004.

Between :

Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad.

Petitioner

and

Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s),

Sadasivpet Municipality

Respondent

I, K. Vema Reddy, S/o Sri K. Obula Reddy, aged about 56 years, residing at Hyderabad do hereby solemnly affirm and state as follows:

I am working as Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad and as such I am well acquainted with the facts of this case.

1. It is respectfully submitted that, one Sri B. Janardhan, while he was working as Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality was trapped by the A.C.B. authorities, when he demanded and accepted a bribe of an amount of Rs.1,000/- from Sri B. Ravi for showing official favour of processing the application of the complainant's father for the issue of house ownership certificate. In the phenolphthalein test conducted in right hand fingers of the Accused Officer yielded positive result and a case has been registered on 9-2-2004 vide reference No.5/ACB-NZB/2004 under Section 7 of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

2 It is further respectfully submitted that, after receipt of automex message from A.C.B. authorities, the petition has carefully examined keeping in view of

the rule position and having due regard in the circumstances of the case, it is necessary to place the Accused Officer under suspension on the public interest, since the individual is involved in case of moral turpitude, pending investigation/trail, as his continuation in the office will be detrimental to the investigation/trail.

3. It is further respectfully submitted that, being appointing authority in respect of Junior Assistants as per Rule 7 of APMMSS Rules issued in G.O.Ms.No.292 M.A., dt. 16-5-92, after careful examination of the matter, and in exercise of the powers conferred by Sub-Rule (1) of Rule 8 of A.P.Civil Services (CCA) Rules, 1991 and Rule 28(3) of APMMSS rules issued in G.O.Ms.No.292 M.A., dt. 16-5-92, the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad has placed Sri M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality under suspension vide proceedings Roc.No.34/2004/A2, dt.17-2-2004. The above orders were served on the Accused Officer on 22.2.2004.

4. It is submitted that, if the Accused Officer may be continued in service, he may be influenced the officials and also there may be chance to tamper the records. Hence, the Accused Officer was suspended with immediately effect.

5. It is further submitted that the Accused Officer may approach the Hon'ble A.P. Administrative Tribunal against the orders of the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad dt. 17-2-2004 in which he was suspended

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ce4e0662-27cb-4092-aa2d-872d5fee5c31/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_318_280_635_1012.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A17%3A11Z%2F-1%2F%2F263ff04be732716574079578bc1e22963fcffdf358b277d6dcf7d6023e25880a" alt="Image" width="9%" /></div>


6. Therefore, it is prayed that this Hon'ble A.P. Administrative Tribunal may kindly issue a notice to the Petitioner (Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad) through the Government Pleader for Home (s), if the Accused Officer files O.A. challenging the above suspension orders and pass such orders as deem fit necessary in the circumstances of the case.

Sworn and signed his name before me on this the ninth day of March, 2004 at Hyderabad.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//ce4e0662-27cb-4092-aa2d-872d5fee5c31/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_2088_2117_2780_2414.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A17%3A11Z%2F-1%2F%2F86ef52d6567e5f1b00f658088717dfd87b04af9bb7ea8b239eee6502e37631e4" alt="Image" width="20%" /></div>

Municipal Administration.

HYGERABAD.

SUB:- PUBLIC SERVICES - Municipal Administration Department - Review of suspension cases for more than eighteen months on 20-1-2006 at 11.30 A.M. - Intimation - Regarding.

-000-

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is informed that a meeting to review the suspension cases pending in Municipal Administration Department for more than eighteen months is fixed on 20-1-2006 at 11.30 A.M. in the Chambers of Secretary to Government (S), Municipal Administration & Urban Development Department (Room No.110, L.Block, A.P.Secretariat). The statement showing the suspension cases pending in the Department for more than eighteen months is enclosed herewith. The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is therefore requested to depute the concerned officials to attend the review meeting with relevant material.

Memo. No. 18113/Vig.I/2003,

Sub: PSs – MA & UD Department – Suspension cases for more than six months Review on 18.8.2006 at 3.30 PM – Intimation – Reg.

*****

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is informed that a meeting to review the suspension cases pending for more than six months is fixed on 18.8.2006 at 3.30 PM in the chambers of Secretary to Government (S), Municipal Administration and Urban Development Department, Room No. 104, L Block, Secretariat. The statement showing the suspension cases (30) pending in the Department for more than six months is enclosed herewith.

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau is therefore requested to depute the concerned officials to attend the review meeting with relevant material.

S.P. SINGH

SECRETARY TO GOVERNMENT

To

The Director General. Anti-Corruption Bureau, Hyderabad (w.e.). Copy to:

P.S. to Secretary to Government (S), MA & UD Department.

// FORWARDED BY ORDER //

C

SECTION OFFICER

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//18f0af1c-16e7-449a-9bc2-99b272f39e97/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_2415_191_3204_880.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A39Z%2F-1%2F%2F57ef963ba944d539e15b64094eb743d56049e6a62cad9ef9a18f749f674371f0" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//18f0af1c-16e7-449a-9bc2-99b272f39e97/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_2415_191_3204_880.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A39Z%2F-1%2F%2F57ef963ba944d539e15b64094eb743d56049e6a62cad9ef9a18f749f674371f0" alt="Image" width="23%" />  794570 DEPARTMENT  </div>   </div>


 $ \underline{\text{Memo. No. 18113/Vig.I/2003}} $,

dated 25.8.2006.

Sub: PSs – MA & UD Department – Suspension cases for more than six months = Review on 4.9.2006 – Intimation – Reg.

Ref: 1. Govt. Memo. No. 18113/Vig.I/2003, dated 11.8.2006.

2. Govt. Memo. No. 18113/Vig.I/2003, dated 17.8.2006.

*****

In continuation to the references cited, the DG, ACB is informed that the meeting for review of the suspension cases for more than six months has been scheduled on 4.9.2006 at 11.30 AM in the chambers of Secretary (S), Municipal Administration and Urban Development Department, L Block, Secretariat. List of cases (revised) for the review is sent herewith.

The Director General, Anti-Corruption Bureau, Hyderabad is therefore requested to depute the concerned to attend the review meeting with relevant material and remarks.

S.P. SINGH

SECRETARY TO GOVERNMENT

To

The Director General, Anti-Corruption Bureau, Hyderabad. Copy to:

All Heads of Departments – With a request to attend the review meeting.

// FORWARDED BY ORDER //

SECTION OFFICER

# PROCEEDINGS OF THE REGIONAL DIRECTOR-CUM-APPELLATE COMMISSIONER OF MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION :: HYDERABAD.

PRESENT : SRI K. VENKATA RAMI REDDY, REGIONAL DIRECTOR.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//4f93ea14-0868-48af-80d3-c0ce86bce85a/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_2332_259_3219_1295.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A13Z%2F-1%2F%2Fcc9037c356590a39d758aff200d05e06228c2ca3de4ca1317fa7aace2bb16a8d" alt="Image" width="26%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//4f93ea14-0868-48af-80d3-c0ce86bce85a/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_2332_259_3219_1295.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A13Z%2F-1%2F%2Fcc9037c356590a39d758aff200d05e06228c2ca3de4ca1317fa7aace2bb16a8d" alt="Image" width="26%" />  E665 8050000 RABAD 21 SEP 2006 A  </div>   </div>


Roc.No.34/2004/A2

Dated: 16-9-2006.

Sub:- Public Services – Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s) – Acquired properties without obtaining permission from the competent authority

Articles of Charges framed - Enquiry Officer appointed - Orders-

Issued.

Read: - 1) Lr. No. 1/1/2004, dt. 10-2-2004 of M.C., Sadasivpet.

2) Progs.Roc.No.34/2004/A2, dt. 17-2-2004 of R.D.M.A., Hyderabad.

3) Lr.Roc.No.C1/MCS/2004, dt. 9-3-2004 of M.C., Sadasivpet addressed to the D.S.P., ACB, Nizamabad and copy marked to this office.

4) Charge Memo Roc.No.34/2004/A2, dt. 23-7-2004 of R.D.M.A., Hyderabad issued to Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s).

5) Explanation dt. 9-8-2004 of Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s).

##### ORDER:-

##### $$$$$$

In the proceedings second read above, Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Sadasivpet Municipality has been kept under suspension due to trapped by the A.C.B. officials in Sadasivpet Municipality.

2. In the Charge memo fourth read above, two charges have been framed against Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s) for acquired properties without obtaining permission from the competent authority.

3. In the reference fifth read above, Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Sadasivpet Municipality has submitted his explanation to the charges framed against him.

5. It is observed by the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Admn., Hyderabad that the reply submitted by the Delinquent Officer, in the reference fifth read above is not proper and convincing.

6. In view of the above, it has been decided to order a detailed enquiry under Rule 20(2) of APCS (CC&A) Rules, 1991 into the irregularities and misconduct of the Delinquent Officer, Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s).

5. Accordingly, Sri M.Parameshwar, Municipal Commissioner, Sadasivpet is appointed as enquiry officer under Rule 20(2) of A.P.Civil Services (CC&A) Rules, 1991 to conduct a detailed enquiry into the charges framed against Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s). A copy of the Charge Memo together with draft articles of

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//4f93ea14-0868-48af-80d3-c0ce86bce85a/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_519_4659_860_5187.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A13Z%2F-1%2F%2F8524dbfff536aa855292d231d1796189472c51c2396edc5a4aef0eb4233e7c27" alt="Image" width="10%" /></div>


charges, statement of imputations, list of documents and list of witnesses and explanation of the Delinquent Officer are herewith enclosed.

6. The Enquiry Officer shall submit his report to this office within 15 days from the date of receipt of this order.

REGIONAL DIRECTOR,

HYDERABAD.

To

Sri M. Parameshwar,

Municipal Commissioner,

Sadasivpet.

Roc.No.34/2004/A2-2

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//4f93ea14-0868-48af-80d3-c0ce86bce85a/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_2465_287_3261_1268.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A21Z%2F-1%2F%2F5f650cf6f5269f24dace10dd0299022d2eebfc0acfc6941ba24d2ee49964d697" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//4f93ea14-0868-48af-80d3-c0ce86bce85a/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_2465_287_3261_1268.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A21Z%2F-1%2F%2F5f650cf6f5269f24dace10dd0299022d2eebfc0acfc6941ba24d2ee49964d697" alt="Image" width="23%" />  06 21 SEP 2006 29.05.  </div>   </div>


Dated: 16-9-2006.

Sub:- Public Services - Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s) - Acquired properties without obtaining permission from the competent authority. Articles of Charges framed - Presenting Officer appointed - Orders-Issued.

Read: - 1) Lr. No.1/1/2004, dt. 10-2-2004 of M.C., Sadasivpet.

2) Progs.Roc.No.34/2004/A2, dt. 17-2-2004 of R.D.M.A., Hyderabad.

3) Lr.Roc.No.C1/MCS/2004, dt. 9-3-2004 of M.C., Sadasivpet addressed to the D.S.P., ACB, Nizamabad and copy marked to this office.

4) Charge Memo Roc.No.34/2004/A2, dt. 23-7-2004 of R.D.M.A., Hyderabad issued to Sri M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s).

5) Explanation dt. 9-8-2004 of Sri M.Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s).

6) Progs. Roc. No. 34/2004/A2-1, dt. 16-9-2006 of R. D. M. A., Hyderabad.

$$$$$

##### ORDER

Whereas, an Enquiry Officer under Rule 20 of A.P.C.S. (CCA) Rules, 1991 has been ordered against Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s) for acquiring properties without obtaining permission from the competent authority..

And whereas, it is considered that Presenting Officer should be appointed to present on behalf of the disciplinary authority the case in support of the article of charges.

Now, therefore, the disciplinary authority in exercise of the powers conferred by Sub-Rule (5) of Rule 20 of the APCS (CC&A) Rules, 1991, hereby appoints Sri M.A.Aleem, Manager, Sadasivpet Municipality as the Presenting Officer.

REGIONAL DIRECTOR, HYDERABAD.

To

Sri M.A. Aleem, Manager,

Sadasivpet Municipality.

(through the Municipal Commissioner, Sadasivpet)

Copy to Sri M. Parameshwar, Enquiry Officer & Municipal Commissioner,

Sadasivpet.

# ANDHRA PRADESH VIGILANCE COMMISSION SECRETARIAT, HYDERABAD. Lr.No:84/VC.D1/04-4, dated:27.10.2006

From

Sri R.C. Samal, IAS(Retd.)

Vigilance Commissioner.

To

The Secy. to Government,

M.A.& U.D.Dept,

Secretariat, Hyderabad. (w.e. and F.R.)

Sir,

Sub: P.S. – Allegation of corruption against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Jr. Asst. O/o Municipal Commr. Sadasivpet, Medak District– Trapped on 9.2.2004 – Final report - Reg.

Ref: From the M.A. & U.D. Department file bearing C.No:3890/Vig.IV.2/04

The file referred to above is returned herewith to the M.A. & U.D. Department.

2. Having examined the material made available in the file, Commission advises the Department to reinstate Sri M. Janardhan, Jr. Asst. into service and transfer him from Medak District and he may be posted to Nalgonda. If he is posted in the nearby municipality, in all probability, he may indulge in same activities again.

3. Receipt of the letter together with the file may please be acknowledged and action taken in the matter may be intimated to the Commission.

Yours faithfully,

For Vigilance Commissioner.

Copy to the Director-General, A.C.B., Hyderabad.

<div style="text-align: center;"></div>

GOVERNMENT OF ANDHRA PRADESH

MUNICIPAL ADMINISTRATION & URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT

MEMO NO.3890/VIG.IV.2/2004-5

DATED: 24-11-

SUB:- Public Services - Allegations of corruption - Demand and acceptance of bribe against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of the Sadasivapet Municipality, Medak District Trapped on 9-2-2004 - Suspension reviewed - Reinstatement - Regarding.

REF:- Proceedings of the RD-cum-AC of M.A., Hyderabad Roc. No.34/2004-A2, dt.17-2-2004.

-000

The Government have reviewed the suspension of Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Sadasivpet Municipality in consultation with appropriate authority and decided to reinstate him into service without prejudice to the criminal/disciplinary proceedings pending against him.

2. The Commissioner and Director of Municipal Administration is therefore advised to reinstate Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant (u/s), Sadasivpet Municipality into service without prejudice to the criminal/disciplinary proceedings pending against him and post him to Nalgonda or anywhere in non focal post where he cannot indulge himself in corrupt practices again.

S.P.SINGH

SECRETARY TO GOVERNMENT

To

The Commissioner and Director of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad.

Copy to:

The Director General, Anti Corruption Bureau, Hyderabad. The A.P.Vigilance Commission

The Regional Director-cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad.

//FORWARDED::BY ORDER//

Sub:- Public Services - Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant (U/s) - Acquired properties without obtaining permission from the competent authority - Departmental Enquiry - Submission of Records/Documents - Reg.

Ref: 1) Lr.No.1/1/2004, Dt. 10-2-2004 of M.C., Sadasivpet.

2) Progs. Roc. No.34/2004/A2, Dt. 17-2-2004 of R.D.M.A., Hyderabad.

3) Endt. No.50/RCT-NMD/2004, Dated 22-9-06 of DG., ACB., A.P., Hyderabad.

With reference to the above subjected cited I am here-with submitting the records of case for the purpose of enquiry.

1) Xerox copy of FIR Cr.NO.5/ACB-NZB/04. 1 sheet.

2) Copy of complainant. 1 sheet.

3) Copy of Part - II Statement of Sri R. Kistaiah, Municipal Commissioner, Sadasivpet. 1 sheet.

4) Copy of Mediator Report - 1 2 sheets

5) Copy of Mediator Report - II 6 Seets.

6) Letter RCNo.C1/MCS/2004, Dt. 9-3-2004 of Municipal Commissioner, Sadasivpet. 1 sheet.

7) Proceedings vide R.C.No. G1/586/2000, Dt. 26-8-2000 of Municipal Commissiones Sadasivpet. 1 sheet.

8) Copy Doc. NO. 1432/2000 in favour of Mamidi Janardhan. 7 Sheets.

9) Copy of Rahul Automobile receipt No.2899., 1 sheet.

10). Gold Mine receipt., 1 sheet.

11) Copy of RTO receipt., 1 sheet.

12) Copy of Rahul Automobile Invoice receipt., 1 sheet.

Encl: Copies of the documents.

2月11日

Tice of the Municipal Council

Sadasivpet.

K. Krishna 110106

Sub:- Public Services - M.A.Dentdr.Assistant, Sadari Suspension Proc. - Dasivpet - Sri M. Janardhan, on 09.09.2004

Ref:- 1. Automex Message Dt.10.02.2004 from the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak.

2. PROgs. Roc. No. 34/2004-A2, Dt. 17.02.2004 of the RDMA, Hyderabad.

3. Your LR. No. 50/RCT-NMD/2004, Dt. 17.02.2004.

4. Progs. Roc. No. 34/2004-A2, Dt. 23.07.2004 of the RDMA, Hyd.

5. Progs. Roc. No. 34/2004-A2, Dt. 23.07.2004 of the RDMA, Hyd.

6. PROGS. ROC. No. 34/2004-A2, Dt. 3011. 2006 of the RDMA, Hyd.

I invite your attention to the references cited. In the reference fifth cited sanction of prosecution orders were issued by the Regional Director-Cum-Appellate Commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad. Hence, I request you to inform the stage of Charge Sheet if any filed in the Court of Anti Corruption Bureau.

Yours faithfully,

Sd/- K.SHIVA PRASAD

ADDITIONAL DIRECTOR

for Commissioner & Director

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//edce41f3-4222-4734-9448-dad079d43afb/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_image_box_1093_3640_1617_4235.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A23Z%2F-1%2F%2Fecc61fdf6c1c3f0fb86941a453c2cab9b08f579cf13fec30bdf177c28936abef" alt="Image" width="15%" /></div>


Sub:- Public Servants --- M. Agent -- Allegations of corruption against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of Sadasivapet Municipality, Medak district --- Trapped on 9-2-2004 --- Stage of the case --- Reg.

Ref: - Lr No.2772/2004-E3, dated 17-12-2007 of the Commissioner & Director of M.A., A.P., Hyderabad.

With reference to the letter cited, it is to inform that the case against Sri Mamidi Janardhan, Junior Assistant of Sadasivapet Municipality, Medak district is pending in the court of Pri. Special Judge for SPE & ACB Cases, Hyderabad vide C.C.No.42/2004 and the same stands posted to 24-1-2008 for examination L.W. 1&4.

The result of the case will be informed after its disposal by the court.

It is further to mention that in this office letter C.No.10/RPC©/2005, dated 5-12-2006 while inform about launching of ACB Website and its utility for 'Online Case Monitoring System', all the Heads of Departments were requested to put it into usage with the 'User ID & Pass Word' allotted to their department for reviewing of ACB Cases/Enquiries pending in different forums viz., Special Courts, High Court, Supreme Court, Tribunal for Disciplinary Proceedings., Commissioner of Inquiries and Departmental enquiries in order to avoid wastage of paper and correspondence with the Bureau and also for updating information in the cases/enquiries that are pending in Departmental Enquiries for quite a long time. Further in Memo No.63/Spl.C/A1/2006-6 dated 21-5-2007 of General Administration (Spl.C) Department, all the Departments of Secretariat are requested to utilize ACB Website to know the status of the cases and also update the stages in Departmental Enquiries initiated in ACB reports and also to direct the Heads of Departments under their respective control to update the cases by making use of the ACB Website.

It is therefore requested to make optimum usage of the Website of the Bureau in future for monitoring the stages of ACB cases/enquiries pending in various forums.

Yours faithfully,

for Director General

Anti-Corruption Bureau

A.P., Hyderabad.

Copy to.

The Vigilance Commissioner, A.P. Vigilance Commission, Hyderabad (Ref: Letter No. 84/VC.D1/04).

The Principal Secretary to Government, Municipal Administration & Urban Development Department, A.P., Hyderabad. (No.3890/Vig.IV.2/2004)

Sir.

Sub:- Public Servants – Acquisition of disproportionate assets against AO Sri M. Janardhan, Jr. Asst. O/o Municipal Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadasiva Pet, Medak. Judgement Pronounced Charges proved – Reg.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a922535f-35fb-4ff4-94e0-8e20c9c8a8bf/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_399_1971_736_2443.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A16Z%2F-1%2F%2Ffc1dce8a10862a1ebf5fd2f93b58718e9ed40344bdf012fc680557c16c4a1d4d" alt="Image" width="9%" /></div>


Ref:- Cr.No. 5/ACB-NZB/04 in CC No. 42 of 2004 on the file of Prl. Spl. Judge for SPE & ACB Cases, Hyderabad.

8&8

Sri N. Sanyasi Rao, Prl., Special Judge for SPE & ACB Cases, Hyderabad pronounced judgment in the reference mentioned case today i.e. on 13-10-2008 convicting Accused Officer Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant O/o Municipal commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadasiva Pet, Medak District. The Hon'ble Judge sentenced the Accused Officer to under go R.I. for Six Months and pay a fine of Rs.5,00/- for the offence U/s. 7 of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 and also under go R.I. for 1 year and pay a fine of Rs.500/- Under Section 13(1)(d) of prevention of Corruption Act, 1988, in default of payment of fine the Accused Officer to suffer one months Simple Imprisonment Both sentences of imprisonment shall run concurrently.

Memo filed in the Honourable court for grant of free copy of judgment and on receipt of the same it would be forwarded to the Head Office.

Submitted for favour of information.

Yours faithfully,

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a922535f-35fb-4ff4-94e0-8e20c9c8a8bf/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_377_4185_1308_5231.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A16Z%2F-1%2F%2F6d2b1c34dc055a51626b5eccbb5a5330fbb6faaf664aea753da0e6f55282e159" alt="Image" width="27%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a922535f-35fb-4ff4-94e0-8e20c9c8a8bf/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_377_4185_1308_5231.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A16Z%2F-1%2F%2F6d2b1c34dc055a51626b5eccbb5a5330fbb6faaf664aea753da0e6f55282e159" alt="Image" width="27%" />  Lector Anti-Corruption Burea 13 OCT 2008  </div>   </div>

# JUNIOR ASSISTANT CONVICTED IN A.C.B. TRAP CASE

The Special Judge for S.P.E. & A.C.B. Cases, Hyderabad convicted Jormel

Sri M. Janardhan, Junior Assistant, Office of the Municipal Commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadasivpet, Medak district on 13-10-2008 and sentenced him to undergo Six months rigorous imprisonment and to pay a fine of Rs.500/- and in default, to suffer one month simple imprisonment for the offence punishable under section 7 of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988, and also under go Rd. for 1 year and pay a fine of Rs.500/- u/s

13(1) (d) of P.C.Act, 1988, in default of payment of fine, the A.G. to suffer one month simple imprisonment. Both sentences of imprisonment shall run concurrently.

##### le

Earlier, the said Junior Assistant was trapped on 9-2-2004 by the Officials of the

A.C.B. when he demanded and accepted a bribe amount of Rs.1,000/- from the

complainant for doing an official favour of processing his application for Ownership

Certificate.

CC No.42 of 2004

# IN THE COURT OF THE PRLSPL.JUDGE FOR SPE & ACB CASES. CITY CIVIL COURT: AT: HYDERABAD.

# DATED THIS THE 13th DAY OF October. 2008

# PRESENT: SRI N.SANYASI RAO. B.Com.LL.M.PGDPR. PRL SPL JUDGE FOR SPE & ACB CASES

##### CALENDAR CASE No.42 OF 2004

Name of the complainant

State, ACB rep. by Inspector of Police, Anti Corruption Bureau, Nizamabad Range, Nizamabad.

Name of the Accused

Sri. Mamidi Janardhan, S/o. Narsimulu, Junior

Assistant, O/O. Municipal Commissioner,

Mumitpal Caemil, Sadashivapet, Medak District, R/o.H.No.6-3-34/4/1, Raghavendra Colony, Sadashivapet, Medak District.

Crime Number

5/ACB-NZB/2004

Offences

U/secs.7, 13(2) R/W. 13(1),(d) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988

Plea of the Accused Officers Pleaded not gu

Finding of the Court

Sentence

Found guilty.

In the result, Accused Officer is found guilty of the charges framed against him under Section-7 and Sec.13(1)(d) r/w Sec.13(2) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 and that he is convicted under section 249 (2) C.P.C.

The accused officer is sentenced to undergo R.I. for period of six months and to pay a fine of Rs.500/- in default to undergo S.I. for a period of one month for the offence punishable under section 7 of the Prevention of corruption Act. 1998.

Further the accused officer is further sentenced to under go R.I. for a period of one year and to pay a fine of Rs.500/- in default to under go S.I. for a period for one month for the offence punishable under section 13(1)(d) r/w sec.13(2) of Prevention of Corruption Act. 1988.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a922535f-35fb-4ff4-94e0-8e20c9c8a8bf/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_295_4265_1092_5147.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A30Z%2F-1%2F%2F5809747a793533bc22857bb567c8b4f187d3dc2d69418c3d579034380d1ef027" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//a922535f-35fb-4ff4-94e0-8e20c9c8a8bf/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_295_4265_1092_5147.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A30Z%2F-1%2F%2F5809747a793533bc22857bb567c8b4f187d3dc2d69418c3d579034380d1ef027" alt="Image" width="23%" />  中国银行股份有限公司  </div>   </div>


Both the sentences of imprisonment of the accused officer shall run concurrently. The remand period if any, shall be given set off U/s. 428 Cr.P.C.

MO 1 tainted amount of Rs.1,000/- shall be returned to PW 1 and that MOs 2 to 6 shall be destroyed after appeal time is over

CC No. 42 of 2014

This case coming on this day for final disposal before me in the presence of the Special Public prosecutor for the complaint and of Srt. T. Vlvek Vardhan Reddy. Counsel for Accused Officer and the matter having stood over for consideration till this day, this Court delivered the following:-

##### JUDGMENT

The Inspector of Police, Anti Corruption Bureau,

Nizamabad range, Nizamabad filed the charge sheet against the

accused officer alleging as follows:

That the accused officer Sri Mamidi Janaradhan worked as Junior Assistant in the office of the Municipal commissioner, Municipal Council, Sadastvapet, Medak district from 5/12/2003 to 22/2/2004 and as such he is a public servant within the meaning of section 2(c) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988; that on 6/2/2004 at about 10.00 a.m. the complainant Sri B.Ravd (Who will be hereinafter referred to as PW1) came to the office of the Inspector of Police, ACB, Medak district at Sanga Reddy, Nizamabad range and gave a written complaint in Telugu, alleging that he is working as a School Assistant in Government Ravindra Model school, Sadastvapet; that his father Sri Bodapally Shankarappa is a retired teacher; that they own a house bearing No.3-4-30 at Yedla Bazar in ward No.11 of Sadastvapet municipality and that his father gave an application on 2/1/2004 in the municipal office, Sadastvapet for issue of house ownership certificate pertaining to his house; that

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_317_4513_1166_5332.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A36Z%2F-1%2F%2Ff0d4636bbce1fce11a530742bc1122f3a6838044a3c5022c12db9267d7581800" alt="Image" width="25%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_317_4513_1166_5332.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A36Z%2F-1%2F%2Ff0d4636bbce1fce11a530742bc1122f3a6838044a3c5022c12db9267d7581800" alt="Image" width="25%" />  TSEA AO E  </div>   </div>


CC No. 42 of 2004

due to the old age of his father, PW 1 approached the accused officer who is a concerned clerk for the issuance of house ownership certificate; that the accused officer demanded Rs.1000/- as bribe to process his father's application; that later again when PW1 met the accused officer for the certificate, he(AO) ascertained from him (PW 1) whether he (PW 1) brought the demanded bribe and further stated that the work would be done as and when the bribe was paid; that finally the accused officer stated that unless the bribe amount was paid, the application would not be processed; that PW 1 informed the same facts to his father; that his father was not willing to get his work done by paying bribe; hence PW 1 came to the ACB office, Sanga Reddy on 6/2/2004 and gave a report to the inspector of Police, ACB, Sanga Reddy, Nizamabad range and requested to take necessary action against the accused officer; that on receiving the report of PW 1 and after due verification, the inspector of Police, ACB, Sanga Reddy registered the report of PW 1 as a case in Cr. No. 5/ACB-NZB/2004 under section 7 of Prevention of corruption Act, 1988 on 9/2/2004 at 9.00 a.m. against the accused officer and took up the investigation; that during the course of investigation, the Inspector of Police, ACB, Sanga Reddy at the presence of two government servants i.e. Sri Padma

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_seal_box_215_4590_1030_5354.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A40Z%2F-1%2F%2F9c6a07bbc8066047595fa22d6cbc625d5198e999f10cf62cc5761204191b1e7a" alt="Image" width="24%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_seal_box_215_4590_1030_5354.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A40Z%2F-1%2F%2F9c6a07bbc8066047595fa22d6cbc625d5198e999f10cf62cc5761204191b1e7a" alt="Image" width="24%" />  secured the   </div>   </div>


4

CC No. 4101 2004

Narayana. Assistant Grade I in the office of the DM. Civil Supplies department. Sanga Reddy and Sri Tammala Shivatah Bal Raj. Assistant Grade III in the office of the DM. Civil Supplies department. Sanga Reddy as mediators and after observing the necessary formalities, the Inspector of Police, ACB. Sanga Reddy with the assistance of Inspector ACB. Nizamabad range. Nizamabad and others laid the trap on 9/2/2004 at 12.40 p.m. at Municipal office staff room. Sadavstvapet. Medak District when the accused officer demanded and accepted the bribe amount of Rs.1000/- from PW1; that the phenolphthalein tested conducted on the right hand fingers of the accused officer yielded positive result and the tainted amount was recovered at his instance from the right side pant pocket of the accused officer; that the accused officer was arrested and produced before the court for remand; that the accused officer was remanded by the court and later released on bail; that during the course of investigation, the inspector of Police, ACB. Sanga Reddy examined and recorded the statements of Sri B.Ravi. Sri R.Kisitalah and Sri B.Satyanarayana; that the inspector of Police, ACB Nizamabad range. Nizamabad examined and recorded the statements of B.Shankarappa and Smt. Sheelam.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_197_4231_993_5040.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A44Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe580cdd0960cd7cc58b45c69324197491c3a4b4f63f68e6bb95667739156a8f2" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_197_4231_993_5040.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A44Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe580cdd0960cd7cc58b45c69324197491c3a4b4f63f68e6bb95667739156a8f2" alt="Image" width="23%" />  Panna C  </div>   </div>


CC No. 42 of 2004

original application of PW 1 along with note file, consisting of 5 sheets; miscellaneous receipt book; inward register and the attendance register of staff of Municipal Council, Sadastvapet; that during the course of investigation, the inspector of Police, ACB, Sanga Reddy filed a requisition before the Hon'ble, Judicial First Class magistrate, Sanga Reddy medak district and the statement of PW 1 under section 164 Cr.P.C. was recorded by Judicial First Class Magistrate, Sanga Reddy medak district; that the Regional Director cum appellate commissioner of Municipal Administration, Hyderabad being the competent authority to remove the accused officer from service had accorded sanction vide Proceedings ROC No. 34/2004/A2, dated 24/7/2004 to prosecute the accused officer in a court of law for the aforesaid offence and for taking cognizance of the offence; thus the facts and material gathered during the course of investigation revealed that the accused officer demanded and accepted brine of Rs.1000/- on 9/2/2004 from PW 1 as a motive for showing official favour of processing the application of Sri B. Shankarappa for issuing house ownership certificate; that the accused officer has committed the offence punishable under section 7 and 13(1)(d) r/w sec. 13(2) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988. Hence the charge.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_seal_box_61_4604_845_5421.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A47Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb0e54a4ec9b76cd3939cd9d9914e7cb57a316535a32f1bcd0e7fe84d3db1bb8a" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_seal_box_61_4604_845_5421.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A47Z%2F-1%2F%2Fb0e54a4ec9b76cd3939cd9d9914e7cb57a316535a32f1bcd0e7fe84d3db1bb8a" alt="Image" width="23%" />  A11 CIF B A 100  </div>   </div>


CC No. 42 of 2004

2. The charges under section 7 and Sec.13(1)(d) r/w

sec.13(2) of Prevention of Corruption Act. 1988 against the

accused officer was framed and explained to him and he

pleaded not guilty and claimed to be tried.

3. The prosecution to prove the guilt of the accused officer examined PWs 1 to 7 and got marked Ex.P.1 to P.11 and MOs 1 to 6. After closing the prosecution side evidence, the accused officer was examined under section 313 Cr.P.C. The accused officer denied the incriminating material appearing against him in the evidence of the prosecution witnesses. The accused officer also filed his written statement. The accused officer also got examined DW 1 and 2 and got marked Ex.D.1 on his behalf.

4. The accused officer filed his written statement alleging that he has been falsely implicated in this case for the reasons that both PW 1 and 2 are anemically disposed towards him; as they were under mistaken impression that he was instrumental in grant of permission to Basveshwar temple committee for the construction of the compound wall around the temple lands which they used to use as passage to go to their house, as such he solemnly deny the charge; that in fact PW 2 has submitted the application under Ex.P.1 in the office on 2/1/2004 for grant of ownership certificate; that from 1/1/2004 he along with other

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_235_4735_1038_5321.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2F0825c9203ecc0542bf9bcdc317f62deb98b36a2c400f8077ac42ebedc2dac927" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//8bf6e4a7-2b69-4b87-8895-0f81167563ae/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_235_4735_1038_5321.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A51Z%2F-1%2F%2F0825c9203ecc0542bf9bcdc317f62deb98b36a2c400f8077ac42ebedc2dac927" alt="Image" width="23%" />  CPLE  </div>   </div>


staff of the municipality was deputed for verification of electoral rolls to find out the dead, migrated voters in the voters list and residual votes list qualifying date as on 1/1/2004 as ordered by the District collector. Medak as such there occurred certain amount of delay in attending to the regular office work, much less the work relating to the ownership certificate to PW 1, and there is no willful default on his part; that on the trap day incident at around 12.45 p.m. he put up the file relating to PW 1 before the commissioner who has approved the same, thereafter prepared the certificate obtained the signature of the commissioner, while he came out of the room of the commissioner, he found PW 1 in the office as while he was handing over the certificate, PW 1 thrusted a wad of currency into his right pant pocket, upon that he hold the hands of PW1 with his right hand, whisking away his right hand with his hands, PW 1 hurriedly went out: that thereafter the ACB Inspector and others came into the office accosted him, searched his pant and took out the said wad of currency notes from his right side pant pocket; that when asked by the Inspector of Police, ACB he has also informed him that PW 1 had thrusted the notes into his right side pant pocket, however,

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_1_4563_770_5395.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A14Z%2F-1%2F%2F1b9beb51e8e5d8238d3d8f00db1d95809430eef67b952baf3c72a3dab1845b65" alt="Image" width="22%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_1_4563_770_5395.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A14Z%2F-1%2F%2F1b9beb51e8e5d8238d3d8f00db1d95809430eef67b952baf3c72a3dab1845b65" alt="Image" width="22%" />  中华人民共和国国务院  </div>   </div>


CC No. 42 of 200

proceedings inspite of his protest; that he is the victim of sinister

and malicious scheme of PW 1, that absolutely there is no

believable evidence on record to substantiate the charge against

him and as such he may acquit him of the charges.

5. Heard both sides.

6. Now the points that arise for consideration are:

1. Whether the accused officer was the public servant

within the meaning of section 2(c) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988.

2. Whether Ex. P.11 sanction order issued by Sri K. Venkata Rami Reddy, Regional director-cum- appellate commissioner of Municipal Administration. Hyderabad is in accordance with the law?

3. Whether the trap proceedings conducted by PW 6 are true, valid and in accordance with the procedure contemplated under law?

4. Whether the accused officer demanded and accepted

the amount of Rs.1000/- from PW 1 on 9/2/2004

at about 12.40 p.m. at Municipal office staff room.

Sadasivapet, Medak district as gratification other than

legal remuneration for doing official favour i.e. for

processing the application of PW 2 for issuing house

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_seal_box_213_4188_899_5000.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A17Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe9346b84e667dc700271f0b7d0ba525129d9abf8f3634fbb0fa904c7636cc043" alt="Image" width="20%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_seal_box_213_4188_899_5000.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A17Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe9346b84e667dc700271f0b7d0ba525129d9abf8f3634fbb0fa904c7636cc043" alt="Image" width="20%" />  中国银行股份有限公司  </div>   </div>


CC No. 42 of 2004

ownership certificate?

5. Whether the prosecution has established the

offences punishable under section 7 and 13(1)(d) r/w

sec. 13(2) of Prevention and Corruption Act, 1988

against the accused officer.

6. POINT NO:1: The learned special public prosecutor argued that the accused officer worked as Junior Assistant in the office of the Municipal Commissioner, Municipal council, Sadashivapet, Medak district from 5/12/2003 to 22/2/2004. Therefore the accused officer was the public servant within the meaning of section 2(c) of Prevention of Corruption Act. 1988. The learned counsel appearing for the accused officer has not much stressed on this point i.e. that the accused officer was not the public servant during the relevant period. However on perusal of section 2(c) (viii) of PC Act. 1988 envisages that any person who holds an office by virtue of which he is authorised or required to perform any public duty. When a person discharging public duties, his service or pay of the Government or remunerated by the Government by fees or commitsston for the performance of any public servant. The definition of the public servant is widely given in the section 2(c) under Clause (1) to (xii) of PC Act. 1988. Section 2(c) (iii) of PC Act. 1988 envisages

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_7_4464_804_5288.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A20Z%2F-1%2F%2F79d34ec3dec93211b27282315d2856f3aa67b7c70429452717a9f76014963818" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_7_4464_804_5288.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A20Z%2F-1%2F%2F79d34ec3dec93211b27282315d2856f3aa67b7c70429452717a9f76014963818" alt="Image" width="23%" />  CHINA ACB C  </div>   </div>


that any person in the service or pay of a Corporation established by or under a Central, provincial or State Act, or an Authority or a body owned or controlled or aided by the Government or a Government company as defined in Section 617 of the Companies Act, 1956. Ex.P.11 is the the prosecution sanction order dated 24/7/2004 issued by Sri K. Venkata Rami Reddy, Regional Director cum appellate commissioner of Municipal Administration. Hyderabad also shows that the accused officer is the public servant within the meaning of section 2(c) of PC Act, 1988. Therefore from the contents Ex.P.11, prosecution sanction order shows that the accused officer is the public servant within the meaning of section 2(c) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988. More over, the accused officer has not denied that he is not the public servant during the relevant period. Therefore the accused officer is the public servant within the meaning of section 2(c) of Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988. This point is answered accordingly.

7. POINT NO:2: The learned special public prosecutor argued that Ex.P.11 sanction order to prosecute the accused officer was obtained from the competent authority by the Investigating Officer. He further argued that the sanction order was obtained as per section 19 of the Prevention of Corruption

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_seal_box_346_4709_1158_5272.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A23Z%2F-1%2F%2F19574682c9cd3e9489e78172696c1a69402729475ed9d7e6e6035c936e416b70" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_seal_box_346_4709_1158_5272.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A23Z%2F-1%2F%2F19574682c9cd3e9489e78172696c1a69402729475ed9d7e6e6035c936e416b70" alt="Image" width="23%" />  002  </div>   </div>


CC No.42 of 2004

Act. 1988 from the competent authority who has power to remove the accused officer and as such Ex. P. 11 sanction order was the valid document as per section 76 of Indian Evidence Act. On the other hand, the learned counsel appearing for the accused officer argued that Ex.P.11 sanction order was not valid one and it was issued without application of mind. Ex.P.11 sanction order contain two pages order issued by Srl K.Venkata Raml Reddy, Regional Director cum appellate commissioner of municipal Administration, Hyderabad. At page No.2 of Ex.P.141 sanction order. It is mentioned that after fully and carefully examining the material placed before him, in respect of the case consider the said aforesaid allegations and having regard to the circumstances of the case consider that the accused officer be prosecuted in a competent Court of law for the afore said offences. In exercise of the powers conferred by section 19(1)(c) of the Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 accorded permission to prosecute the accused officer for the above said offences. On perusal of Ex.P.11 sanction order, the sanctioning authority has considered all the records sent to them for according permission to prosecute the accused officer. Therefore the sanction order was issued only after applying the mind in this case. Therefore as per the Art.311 of the constitution and as per section 76 of

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_14_4488_770_5303.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A26Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe5295f379967be003b39783ccce02b9ca1a65d92adbdd0b53d38a44a3e69d434" alt="Image" width="22%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//afd4ac25-8b81-49d7-94a0-1ed7a6c868d9/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_14_4488_770_5303.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A26Z%2F-1%2F%2Fe5295f379967be003b39783ccce02b9ca1a65d92adbdd0b53d38a44a3e69d434" alt="Image" width="22%" />  0025  </div>   </div>


CC No. 42 of 2004

Evidence Act, Ex.P.11 sanction order was the valid document.

Apart from, the learned special public prosecutor relied on a

decision reported in (2006) 1 SCC (Crl) 346 (Shankarbat

Laljibhai Rot Vs. State of Gujarat) wherein it was held that;

Sanction order Validity of challenge to - when not

sustainable held that: in the absence of anything to

show that any defect or irregularity in sanction caused

failure of justice, plea relating to sanction was without

substance.

In another decision reported in 1996 Crl.L.J.3638 (AP)

( CBI SPE Hyderabad Vs P.Muthiramani) wherein the Hon'ble

High Court observed that:

Once the signature on the sanction is proved and if the sanction order is a speaking order, then the matter ends there and it presumed that the sanctioning authority has perused the material before according sanction.

In the instant case also, Ex.P.11 sanction order was issued after application of the mind and after gone through the documents sent to Sri K. Venkata Rami Reddy. Regional Director cum appellate commissioner of municipal administration. Hyderabad. Therefore the above cited decision are quite

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_385_4627_1184_5328.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A42Z%2F-1%2F%2F988c3a705091c2296711b300ba2385b1aa3fcea9ed4016c39d4236a457562979" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_seal_box_385_4627_1184_5328.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A42Z%2F-1%2F%2F988c3a705091c2296711b300ba2385b1aa3fcea9ed4016c39d4236a457562979" alt="Image" width="23%" />  Hyderabad T COIL T  </div>   </div>


CC No.42 of 2004

applicable to the facts of the case on hand. Therefore Ex.P.11

sanction order was issued in accordance with the law. Hence

this point is answered in favour of the prosecution as against the

accused officer.

POINT NO: 3 and 4: PW 1 is the defacto complainant.

He deposed that he gave the complaint Ex.P.2 to the Inspector of Police. ACB. Sanga Reddy and that he himself wrote the complaint. He also deposed about pre trap proceedings as well as demand and acceptance of bribe amount by the accused officer on the date of trap incident. PW 2 was the father of PW 1. He deposed that on 2/1/2004 he submitted Ex.P.1 application in the municipal office; that he instructed his son PW 1 to pursue the said application in the municipal office and to obtain ownership certificate; that his son PW 1 informed him that the accused officer was asking Rs.1000/- for himself and that his son PW 1 complained against the accused officer to the ACB. PW 3 was the Assistant Grade I in the office of the District Manager. AP State Civil Supplies corporation. Sanga Reddy during the relevant period who acted as a mediator. He deposed in detail about the pre trap and post trap operations. PW 3 was the Municipal Commissioner. Sadasivapet municipality deposed that on 9/2/2004 at about 12.15 p.m. the accused officer came to his

room and put up a note before me him an on seeing the note he endorsed on the note chit that to collect the fee and issue certificate; that after five minutes the accused officer came to him with a further note informing that he collected Rs.100/- towards fee and he perused the note and also signed on the certificate and that as per the citizen charter within five days of the application the required certificate has to be issued to the concerned. PW 5 was municipal commissioner Sadastvapet from July, 2001 to January, 2004, deposed that the accused officer was the junior assistant in their office; that he received application Ex.P.1 from PW 2 for issue of ownership certificate on 2/1/2004; that he has endorsed Ex.P.1 to revenue section; that the accused officer has to put up the file for issuance of ownership certificate and that the accused did not put up the file before him; and that the ownership certificate has to be given to the intending person within 5 or 6 days in normal course after verifying the records during the relevant period. PW 6 was the Inspector of Police, ACB, Sanga Reddy deposed that he was the trap laying officer and monitored the pre-trap and post trap operations. PW 7 Regional Director cum appellate commissioner, Municipal Administration, Hyderabad deposed that he issued Ex.P.11 sanction proceedings against the accused officer to

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_486_4765_1310_5371.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A50Z%2F-1%2F%2F5a426c411c3c48f29b4ebc01600cfbf856f7da920d0e0ff3c53978475ce30f08" alt="Image" width="24%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_2/imgs/img_in_seal_box_486_4765_1310_5371.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A50Z%2F-1%2F%2F5a426c411c3c48f29b4ebc01600cfbf856f7da920d0e0ff3c53978475ce30f08" alt="Image" width="24%" />  中华人民共和国  </div>   </div>

prosecute him in a court of law for the offences with which the

accused officer was charged.

9. The accused officer was charged for the offences under section 7 and 13(1)(d) r/w section 13(2) of prevention of Corruption Act, 1988. To sustain the charge under section 7 of the Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 against the accused officer, the prosecution has to prove that the accused officer was the public servant at the material time or at the time of the incident. Secondly the accused officer has accepted or obtained gratification other than legal remuneration in respect of official act and thirdly that the gratification was in order to doing official favour i.e. for processing the application of PW 2 for issuing house ownership certificate.

10. Section 13(1)(d) of the prevention of corruption Act.

1988 lays down that any public servant by corrupt or illegal means or by otherwise by abusing his position as public servant obtained for himself or for any other person any valuable thing or pecuniary advantage. The said person would be guilty of criminal misconduct as he is a public servant. The presumption has to be drawn under section 20 of the Prevention of Corruption 1988 in case if the accused officer is a public servant

in case if the accused officer is a public servant

or obtains the gratification other than legal

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_seal_box_74_4451_872_5255.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A54Z%2F-1%2F%2F7a3589bd682494b21e3c5f4e78f461fd826154d72d04b3870676ed2cd4cd52de" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_3/imgs/img_in_seal_box_74_4451_872_5255.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A54Z%2F-1%2F%2F7a3589bd682494b21e3c5f4e78f461fd826154d72d04b3870676ed2cd4cd52de" alt="Image" width="23%" />  SPE 1 2001 2   </div>   </div>


CC No.42 of 2004

<div style="text-align: center;"></div>


remuneration.

11. The first element under section 7 of the Prevention of Corruption Act, 1988 is already concluded in the point No.1. Therefore this aspect need not be discussed at this stage about the sanction orders to accord permission to prosecute the accused officer was issued only after application of the mind and on perusal of the documents sent to the concerned department and satisfied for issuing the same.

12. The learned special public prosecutor argued that the accused officer is working as Junior Assistant in the office of the Municipal office. Sadastvapet: that PW 2 is the owner and father of PW 1; that they applied for ownership certificate which is required to file in the civil suit to prove their right: that PW 2 made an application for issuance of ownership certificate, which is pending till the date of trap incident: that the application is filed on 2/1/2004; that PW 1 stated about the demand made by the accused officer for issuance of ownership certificate: that the accused officer demanded and accepted the tainted amount; that the right hand fingers as well as inner lintings of the pant pocket of the accused officer was proved positive when it was tested with chemicals; that the tainted amount was recovered from the right side pant pocket of the accused officer; that the contention

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_227_4598_1037_5240.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A58Z%2F-1%2F%2Fabbec64101e1cbadea5d1b69c610b3f8d6c46ae40c01385f07b0480d5cb4bde6" alt="Image" width="23%" />  <div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//5bfadd80-83e5-482b-aedb-78dc12523f7c/markdown_4/imgs/img_in_seal_box_227_4598_1037_5240.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-06-04T10%3A16%3A58Z%2F-1%2F%2Fabbec64101e1cbadea5d1b69c610b3f8d6c46ae40c01385f07b0480d5cb4bde6" alt="Image" width="23%" />  C.C.C S.L.  </div>   </div>


CCNo.42of2004

of the defence is that PW 1 and 2 bore grudge against the

accused officer regarding the dispute at school committee, but it

is nothing to do with the trap case; that the evidence of PW 4

and 6 clearly shows that the certificate has to be issued within
